integrated_multimodal_description:

[SHOT 1] — 0.00s–1.80s
“ALL OF THEM”

Continue DIRECTLY from Part 1.

Milo is beside Luma’s enormous paper surface.

ONE glowing paper star is already attached.

His satchel is open and overflowing with more stars.

Pip hovers beside him with huge worried eyes.

Milo grins.

Dialogue:

Pip:
<d>[English] You were serious?</d>

Milo:
<d>[English] Every single one.</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] Oh boy.</d>

Milo reaches into the satchel with both hands.

He pulls out two glowing stars.

He quickly but carefully attaches one.

Then another.

Camera:
close on single Part-1 star
→ satchel overflowing
→ Milo’s excited face
→ close handmade-star placements.

Sound:
- paper rustle
- tiny attachment clicks
- soft golden “ting” on each star
- Pip wings
- gentle lantern hum

Music:
playful pizzicato + marimba begins immediately.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 2] — 1.80s–3.70s
THE TINY THANK-YOU GETS BIGGER

Create a fluid miniature decorating montage.

Do NOT use frantic hard cuts.

Show Milo climbing along Luma’s hanging ribbons and lower structural rim.

He attaches glowing stars in curved patterns across her enormous lantern body.

Pip reluctantly assists by carrying one small star at a time.

Progression should be visible:

few stars
→ cluster
→ glowing trail
→ several clusters.

Milo:
<d>[English] Higher, Pip!</d>

Pip, struggling:
<d>[English] I am a firefly, not a delivery service!</d>

Milo laughs.

Luma remains asleep.

Her internal light slowly becomes slightly warmer as more stars are attached.

Camera:
smooth lateral tracking across Luma
→ close details
→ wider scale shot showing growing patterns.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 3] — 3.70s–5.20s
A LOT BRIGHTER

Continue the montage as Milo finishes a sweeping line of stars.

The star coverage has become spectacular.

Use a beautiful pull-back reveal.

Luma now floats above the city with hundreds of glowing paper-star points wrapping gracefully around her giant lantern body.

Do not completely obscure her cream paper surface.

She should still clearly look like Luma.

Milo sits briefly on a ribbon knot catching his breath.

Pip hovers beside him completely exhausted.

Milo looks up proudly.

Milo:
<d>[English] Okay...</d>

Tiny beat.

<d>[English] Now that looks like a celebration.</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] That looks visible from another country.</d>

Milo giggles.

Camera:
medium on Milo
→ dramatic but gentle pull-back
→ huge wide lantern-over-city reveal.

Sound:
- hundreds of subtle star shimmers
- nighttime city
- ribbons
- Pip exhausted buzz

Music:
warm celebratory flourish.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 4] — 5.20s–6.70s
LUMA STARTS TO WAKE

The music suddenly becomes sparse.

Close-up on Luma’s peaceful face.

Her cheek glows.

Her eyelid makes a tiny twitch.

Milo freezes.

Pip freezes.

Another eyelid twitch.

Luma’s giant eyes slowly open.

Her gaze settles on tiny Milo.

Milo is holding the FINAL remaining star in his hand.

Pip slowly hides behind Milo’s shoulder.

Milo’s face changes:

proud
→ realization
→ nervous.

Dialogue:

Pip, whispering:
<d>[English] You did ask first... right?</d>

Milo:
<d>[English] ...No.</d>

Camera:
Luma eye close-up
→ tiny Milo reflected in giant eye
→ extreme scale shot.

Sound:
- star shimmer reduces
- soft paper movement
- Luma awakening hum
- tiny comedic silence

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 5] — 6.70s–8.20s
“SURPRISE?”

Luma looks at the hundreds/thousands of glowing decorations covering her.

Then she looks back at Milo.

Milo slowly raises the final paper star like an awkward peace offering.

Tiny nervous smile.

Milo:
<d>[English] ...Surprise?</d>

Hold a short silence.

Luma looks at one glowing star.

Then several.

Her expression softens.

Her eyes become warm.

A faint rosy glow appears on her cheeks.

Luma, gentle sleepy voice:
<d>[English] Are these... thank-yous?</d>

Milo nods.

Milo:
<d>[English] For everyone who stayed.</d>

Luma smiles.

Luma:
<d>[English] Then they should see them.</d>

Camera:
three-character scale composition
→ close Milo
→ close Luma’s eyes
→ star detail
→ Luma warm smile.

Music:
emotional theme begins beneath the final line.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 6] — 8.20s–9.80s
LUMA TAKES THE CELEBRATION HIGHER

Luma’s internal amber light grows brighter.

Not blinding.

Her long ribbons gently move.

One ribbon curls beneath Milo and forms a secure supportive loop/seat.

Milo settles safely into it.

Pip hovers beside him.

Luma rises slowly above the rooftop.

The city expands beneath them.

Milo grips the ribbon gently and stares outward.

Dialogue:

Milo:
<d>[English] Whoa...</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] We are very high.</d>

Luma, smiling:
<d>[English] Just high enough.</d>

Camera:
medium Milo supported in ribbon
→ slow crane/pull-back
→ city reveal below.

Sound:
- ribbon movement
- soft wind
- rising lantern hum
- distant city ambience

Music:
gentle magical lift.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 7] — 9.80s–11.40s
THE STARS BEGIN TO LEAVE

Close-up.

One folded paper star on Luma’s surface trembles gently.

Its edge lifts.

The star separates.

It floats upward.

Milo notices.

Then another star lifts.

Then five.

Then dozens.

Milo reaches toward them.

Dialogue:

Milo:
<d>[English] Wait! The stars!</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] They’re flying away!</d>

Luma remains calm.

Luma:
<d>[English] Watch.</d>

More stars gently detach.

They do NOT explode.

They spiral gracefully upward in beautiful curved paths.

Camera follows ONE star first.

Then pull wider as dozens become hundreds.

Stars leave glowing trails against the deep indigo sky.

Sound:
- soft folded-paper flutter
- shimmering rise tones
- wind
- magical constellation ambience

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 8] — 11.40s–13.20s
3,200 LITTLE LIGHTS

The stars rise above the city.

Hundreds become thousands of tiny warm points.

They swirl in elegant coordinated arcs.

At first:
abstract spiral.

Then:
one curved line settles.

Another.

Shapes become recognizable.

Camera pulls VERY wide.

The glowing stars clearly form:

3200

across the night sky.

HOLD THE EXACT NUMBER.

Make it large and unmistakably readable.

Milo stares upward.

His eyes reflect “3200.”

His mouth opens slightly.

He becomes emotionally still.

Pip’s wings slow as he stares.

Luma watches Milo rather than the number.

Milo, barely above a whisper:
<d>[English] Three thousand two hundred...</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] That’s a lot of stars.</d>

Milo:
<d>[English] That’s a lot of people.</d>

Camera:
star swarm
→ formation transition
→ enormous wide “3200”
→ emotional Milo close-up with golden digits reflected in his eyes.

Music:
reach the main emotional theme here.

Do NOT rush this reveal.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 9] — 13.20s–15.00s
THANK YOU — FINAL CELEBRATION

The “3200” constellation gently loosens.

Do NOT dissolve randomly.

Star trails elegantly sweep into new positions.

The lights reform into:

THANK YOU

exactly.

Milo places one hand over his heart.

His eyes become slightly glossy with emotion but he does NOT dramatically cry.

Milo:
<d>[English] Thank you... all of you.</d>

Luma gently lifts Milo from the ribbon and allows him to sit safely on her upper structural rim / top surface.

Pip settles beside him.

The “THANK YOU” formation gradually relaxes back into hundreds of beautiful floating stars.

A subtle heart-shaped star cluster may appear briefly.

The exact “3200” can remain faintly suggested as a distant constellation off to one side, but do not compete with the final characters.

FINAL HERO COMPOSITION:

- giant glowing Luma floating above the city
- Milo sitting safely on top
- Pip beside him
- hundreds of warm paper stars floating around
- distant rooftops and glowing windows below
- deep indigo sky
- crescent moon
- soft golden reflections
- tiny heart/star formations
- peaceful magical atmosphere

Luma softly asks:

<d>[English] Ready for the next one?</d>

Milo smiles.

<d>[English] Always.</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] After a nap.</d>

Milo laughs softly.

Luma smiles.

Hold the warm composition.

FADE TO BLACK.

END.

integrated_multimodal_description:

[SHOT 1] — 0.00s–1.80s
A QUIET ROOFTOP AND 3,200 LITTLE LIGHTS

Open with a beautiful nighttime establishing shot.

Deep indigo sky.

Crescent moon.

Thousands of distant real stars.

Camera slowly descends toward a peaceful rooftop overlooking a softly glowing city.

Milo sits near the rooftop edge.

Beside him is his brown satchel.

Milo opens the satchel.

Pip floats quietly beside him.

wide skyline

Tiny pause.

Milo smiles.

<d>[English] That’s a lot of people.</d>

Pip gives a soft happy chirp.

Sound:

Milo holds the glowing star near his chest.

He looks at the many remaining stars.

Dialogue:

Milo:

Pip tilts his head.

Then Milo notices an enormous warm amber light above him.

The light softly brightens his face.

Milo slowly looks upward.

Camera follows his gaze.

Reveal the lower edge of Luma entering frame.

Long decorative ribbons sway gently beneath her.

Milo’s eyes widen.

Dialogue:

Milo:
<d>[English] Oh...</d>

Pip slowly turns upward too.

Sound:
- gentle ribbon movement
- low warm lantern hum
- night breeze

introduce airy flute and soft wonder motif.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 3] — 3.50s–5.20s
THE SLEEPY SKY LANTERN

Camera continues upward into a full magical reveal of Luma.

Luma floats above the city.

Huge.

Round.

Warmly illuminated.

Her eyes remain peacefully closed.

Her tiny smile is gentle.

She slowly bobs in the night air.

Milo is tiny at the bottom of frame.

This shot must strongly emphasize SCALE.

Milo looks at:
the glowing star in his hand...

then Luma’s enormous blank lantern surface.

Idea forming.

His mouth curls into a grin.

Pip notices Milo’s expression.

Dialogue:

Milo, whispering:
<d>[English] Pip...</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] What?</d>

Milo:
<d>[English] I have an idea.</d>

Pip immediately becomes suspicious.

Pip:
<d>[English] That face worries me.</d>

Music:
small mischievous plucked motif enters.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 4] — 5.20s–7.00s
THE SECRET STAR MISSION

Milo walks beneath one long hanging Luma ribbon.

He grabs it with both hands.

Tests it.

Looks upward.

It extends far above him.

Milo puts the glowing star securely into his hoodie pocket / small pouch while keeping it visible and safe.

Then starts climbing.

Pip hovers beside him.

Dialogue:

Milo, whispering:
<d>[English] We should celebrate.</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] From the roof.</d>

Milo climbs higher.

Milo:
<d>[English] Too ordinary.</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] Of course.</d>

Camera:
low-angle ribbon shot
→ side tracking with Milo climbing
→ pull wide showing tiny Milo beneath giant lantern.

Sound:
- fabric ribbon strain
- Milo’s tiny climbing effort
- shoe taps
- Pip wing buzz
- soft Luma hum

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 5] — 7.00s–9.00s
MILO VS. LUMA

Milo reaches a large tassel knot.

He pauses and sits momentarily on it.

The knot itself is nearly Milo-sized.

He looks upward at the enormous lantern surface.

Pip floats beside him, slightly out of breath despite being a firefly.

Use a vertical scale composition.

Milo climbs the final short ribbon section.

He reaches Luma’s lower lantern edge.

Luma remains completely asleep.

Dialogue:

Milo:
<d>[English] Almost there...</d>

Pip:
<d>[English] Define “almost.”</d>

Milo pulls himself onto a decorative rim.

Dialogue:

Milo:
<d>[English] There.</d>

Sound:
- ribbon movement
- tiny climb grunt

- soft floating ambience

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 6] — 9.00s–11.00s
THE FIRST STAR

Milo retrieves the ONE glowing folded star.

He looks at it.

Then at Luma’s huge lantern body.

He carefully presses the handmade star onto the lantern using a tiny clip / adhesive tab.

Take time with this.

The star gently settles.

For half a beat:

nothing.

Then its glow becomes slightly warmer.

The golden star light blends beautifully with Luma’s amber paper body.

Milo’s face lights up.

Pip watches cautiously.

Milo:

--------------------------------------------------

ONE VERY BIG SUCCESS

Milo leans back against Luma’s decorative rim.

<d>[English] Okay...</d>

<d>[English] That is actually nice.</d>

Milo beams.

+

+

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 8] — 12.80s–15.00s
THE MUCH BIGGER IDEA — CLIFFHANGER

Milo’s proud smile suddenly pauses.

He remembers something.

He looks downward.

Camera follows his gaze toward the rooftop.

His satchel remains there / hangs securely nearby according to spatial setup, visibly overflowing with glowing paper stars.

Alternatively, if he carried the satchel during the climb, show it hanging securely from his shoulder and still overflowing.

CRITICAL:
do not teleport the satchel.

Use whichever configuration is established earlier and maintain it consistently.

Milo slowly opens / looks into it.

Dozens upon dozens of glowing folded stars illuminate his face.

Then he looks at:

the full satchel...

the ONE star on Luma...

Luma’s ENORMOUS remaining lantern surface.

Milo’s eyes widen.

A mischievous smile grows.

Pip looks at Milo.

Pip looks at the satchel.

Pip looks at enormous Luma.

Pip’s expression turns to immediate horror.

Pip:
<d>[English] Milo...</d>

Milo raises the satchel slightly.

Milo, delighted whisper:
<d>[English] Maybe... all of them?</d>

Pip’s eyes become huge.

Pip:
<d>[English] ALL?!</d>

Luma continues peacefully sleeping.

The ONE attached star glows softly.

Hold on:

Milo = enormous inspiration
Pip = comic panic
Luma = peacefully unaware

CUT TO BLACK.

END PART 1.

--------------------------------------------------

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Create a 15 second, 16:9 luxury fashion commercial using @Image1@Image2 as exact visual references. @Image1 is the sole product reference. Preserve the bag’s exact proportions,...</summary>

~~~~text
Create a 15 second, 16:9 luxury fashion commercial using @Image1@Image2 as exact visual references. @Image1 is the sole product reference. Preserve the bag’s exact proportions, pale grey white woven textile, irregular rectangular stitching, soft structure, curved flap, silver clasp, four black eyelets, side construction, zipper details, and hardware. Do not reinterpret it as leather. The bag has two separate short straps made from silver chain woven with matching textile. Preserve their exact construction, attachment points, resting positions, and maximum lengths. The front strap connects only the two front eyelets. The rear strap connects only the two rear eyelets. They must never cross, merge, disappear, duplicate, lengthen, or be rerouted. @Image2 is the exact character reference. Preserve the woman’s face, green eyes, straight blonde bob, natural proportions, and understated makeup. Dress her consistently in a sophisticated beige silk summer mini dress with a square neckline, narrow shoulder straps, fitted waist, and softly moving skirt. Add ivory slingback heels and small pearl earrings. The bag is the visual anchor throughout. It must always be the largest, brightest, sharpest, or most prominently framed element. The woman, hotel, landscape, and car are supporting elements only.
0 to 1.75 seconds: Tight product shot of the bag alone on a sunlit limestone console beside a softly shimmering Riviera pool. Directional sunlight reveals the textile weave, stitching, two straps, four eyelets, side details, and clasp. 1.75 to 3.25 seconds: Keep the bag large and tack sharp in the foreground while the woman approaches through the bright hotel suite in soft focus. She has not touched it yet. 3.25 to 5 seconds: Extreme close up of her fingers lifting both straps together. Keep the front and rear straps visibly separate, correctly routed, and uncrossed. Show realistic chain tension, textile threaded through the links, and accurate hands. 5 to 7 seconds: She places both short straps together over one shoulder and wears the bag as a side shoulder bag. It hangs close beneath her arm, high against the side of her upper waist. It must never reach her hip or thigh. Both straps remain visibly separate, uncrossed, and at their true maximum length. 7 to 9 seconds: Close tracking shot through a sunlit hotel colonnade, composed mainly around the bag moving naturally against the beige silk dress. The straps respond realistically to her steps, shoulder movement, tension, and gravity. 9 to 11 seconds: The bag stands upright and tack sharp on a pale stone terrace table. Its two straps settle naturally and remain identifiable. The woman sits behind it in soft focus, looking toward the sea. 11 to 13 seconds: She carries the bag on one shoulder toward a cream vintage convertible. In a tight product focused montage, she opens the driver door, removes both straps from her shoulder, and carefully places the bag upright on the front passenger seat cushion. The bag’s entire base must rest directly on the horizontal cushion where a passenger sits. It is not on the backrest, headrest, seat edge, center console, or top of the seat. The passenger backrest rises behind it. Both straps settle naturally beside and partly behind the bag without disappearing. She enters the driver seat. Keep the car cropped and secondary. 13 to 15 seconds: The car moves along the Riviera coast. Keep the bag upright, large, and tack sharp on the passenger seat cushion while the woman holds steering wheel and drives in the soft background. The sea and road pass through the windows in gentle motion blur. Moving sunlight reveals the woven textile, stitching, eyelets, two distinct straps, and hardware. End with one controlled flash across the silver clasp.
Use bright, fashion forward European luxury cinematography inspired by a restored 1990s campaign film. Directional Mediterranean sunlight, dimensional shadows, layered reflections, warm 35 mm grain, gentle haliation, creamy highlights, subtle film weave, natural motion blur, and tactile textile detail. Use elegant editorial cuts, realistic movement, accurate hands, and consistent spatial logic. Add a sophisticated original fashion score with crisp percussion, warm bass, airy electronic textures, subtle chain sounds, clasp click, silk movement, footsteps, coastal ambience, car door movement, and quiet engine sound. No dialogue. No product redesign, leather texture, colour change, altered stitching, missing or merged straps, crossed straps, extra straps, extended straps, changed eyelet routing, crossbody carry, low hip placement, warped chains, changed hardware, duplicated bag, distorted hands, outfit changes, blue dress, car hero shot, wide vehicle profile, empty road shot, incorrect seat placement, text, or subtitles.
~~~~

[REFERENCE CONTROL]

Use the uploaded PART 2 storyboard image for “The Tiny Florist and the Sleeping Mountain Giant” as the PRIMARY visual, character, environment, scale, prop, color, performance, and narrative reference.

This sequence continues DIRECTLY from PART 1.

PART 1 ended with:
- Brum still peacefully asleep beneath the ancient tree
- exactly ONE small flower already tucked into his enormous beard
- Mimi discovering that her woven flower basket is still completely full
- Mimi getting an excited “bigger makeover” idea
- Buzz realizing exactly where this is going and saying “NO.”

Generate a FINISHED cinematic animation from this storyboard.

DO NOT reproduce the storyboard sheet itself.

The final video must NOT contain:
- storyboard borders
- panel numbers
- timestamps
- handwritten captions
- dialogue bubbles
- storyboard headers
- production notes
- character reference drawings
- scale charts
- paper texture
- footer decorations

The uploaded storyboard locks:

1. Mimi’s exact identity and costume
2. Buzz’s exact design and goggles
3. Brum’s exact giant face, beard, vest and proportions
4. extreme tiny-vs-giant scale
5. existing single flower from Part 1
6. progressive flower-beard makeover
7. pink petal approaching Brum’s nose
8. giant harmless sneeze
9. Mimi safely holding one beard strand
10. Brum waking gently
11. Mimi’s tiny “Surprise?” reaction
12. Brum lifting Mimi safely onto his palm
13. pond reflection reveal
14. Brum loving the makeover
15. final Buzz “NO.” joke
16. squirrel relaxing inside the finished beard
17. final magical forest hero shot

Translate the 15 storyboard panels into approximately 9 fluid cinematic shots.

DO NOT create fifteen rapid one-second cuts.

Prioritize:
clear cause → action → consequence → reaction → emotional payoff.

--------------------------------------------------

[VIDEO GOAL]

Duration:
EXACTLY 15 seconds

Story time:
0:15–0:30

Aspect Ratio:
16:9 landscape

Frame-rate feeling:
cinematic 24 fps

Visual medium:
premium painterly 2D animated short film

Genre:
whimsical fantasy comedy + heartwarming friendship

Audience:
family-friendly

Dialogue:
natural synchronized English

Audio:
native dialogue + SFX + forest ambience + original music

EMOTIONAL ARC:

creative obsession
→ escalating makeover comedy
→ proud reveal
→ suspicious nose twitch
→ tiny petal suspense
→ enormous harmless sneeze
→ awkward “caught” moment
→ giant discovers makeover
→ unexpected emotional appreciation
→ joyful friendship
→ final comedy button

The finished 30-second story should feel COMPLETE at the end.

--------------------------------------------------

[PART 2 CORE STORY]

Mimi decides one flower is not enough.

She decorates Brum’s beard with:
- daisies
- pink blossoms
- little yellow flowers
- small vines
- tiny mushrooms
- one tiny sunflower

The enormous messy beard becomes a whimsical miniature beard garden.

Mimi proudly admires her masterpiece.

Buzz notices Brum’s nose twitching.

A loose pink petal drifts toward Brum’s nostril.

The petal tickles his nose.

Brum inhales.

Mimi and Buzz realize what is about to happen.

Brum sneezes.

The huge but harmless sneeze sends petals swirling through the air.

Mimi grabs one enormous beard strand safely.

Buzz tumbles comedically.

Brum wakes.

Mimi nervously says:

<d>[English] Surprise?</d>

Instead of getting angry, Brum discovers his decorated beard.

He sees himself reflected in a nearby pond.

He loves it.

He tells Mimi no one has decorated his beard in a hundred years.

Then Brum asks Buzz if he helped.

Buzz instantly replies:

<d>[English] NO.</d>

End with Brum, Mimi, Buzz and the squirrel happily together.

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — MIMI]

Match PART 1 and the uploaded PART 2 storyboard EXACTLY.

MIMI:

Appearance:

- soft rounded face
- fluffy chestnut-brown short bob
- natural loose strands

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — BRUM]

--------------------------------------------------

[BEARD GARDEN CONTINUITY]

At the first frame:

Brum’s beard still has EXACTLY the single flower from Part 1.

Build progressively.

STAGE 1:

STAGE 2:

STAGE 3:

finished beard garden

- white daisies
- pink blossoms
- little yellow flowers
- small lavender flowers
- green vines
- tiny red-and-white mushrooms
- one small sunflower

Do NOT cover Brum’s entire beard with flowers.

It must still clearly be a beard.

--------------------------------------------------

[FLOWER PHYSICS]

Flowers are real lightweight flowers.

They should:
- bend softly
- move with Brum’s breathing
- react naturally to breeze
- shed only a few petals during sneeze

No glowing magical flowers.

No instant plant growth.

Mimi physically decorates the beard.

--------------------------------------------------

[KEY SNEEZE PETAL LOCK]

Use ONE clearly readable soft pink petal.

The suspense sequence must clearly show:

→ Brum’s nose twitches
→ pink petal loosens
→ petal floats upward
→ petal approaches nostril
→ touches / tickles nose
→ Brum inhales
→ SNEEZE.

Do not make the sneeze happen randomly.

The viewer must understand the cause.

--------------------------------------------------

[SNEEZE SAFETY RULE]

The sneeze is the largest comedy action of the film, but it is completely harmless.

The gust may cause:
- flower petals to swirl
- loose leaves to fly
- Mimi’s hair to blow backward
- Mimi’s apron to flutter
- Buzz to spin through petals
- butterflies to scatter
- light flowers to shake

Mimi grabs one thick beard strand before being moved far.

She NEVER falls.

Buzz NEVER crashes painfully.

Brum’s beard remains intact.

No trees break.

No objects become dangerous projectiles.

No injuries.

No destruction.

--------------------------------------------------

[ENVIRONMENT LOCK]

Use the same enchanted forest clearing as Part 1.

- golden sunlight
- ferns
- wildflowers
- mushrooms
- stones
- leafy canopy
- atmospheric forest depth
- warm moss
- nearby reflective pond
- drifting petals
- Brum’s same resting position

The pond should already exist spatially nearby so Brum can see his reflection naturally after waking.

Do not teleport characters to another location.

--------------------------------------------------

[SCALE RULES]

Scale is CRITICAL.

Mimi = approximately Brum’s thumb height.

Buzz = Mimi-head-sized.

Squirrel = slightly larger than Mimi.

Brum = colossal.

Visual examples:

- Brum’s eye is larger than Mimi
- Brum’s nose towers above Mimi
- one beard strand is thick enough for Mimi to grip with both hands

Maintain scale even during close-ups.

[MOTION RULES — BRUM]

After:

[REFERENCE CONTROL]

Use the uploaded PART 1 storyboard image for “The Tiny Florist and the Sleeping Mountain Giant” as the PRIMARY visual, character, environment, scale, prop, performance, color, and narrative reference.

Generate a FINISHED animated sequence from the storyboard.

1. Mimi’s exact visual identity
2. Brum’s exact giant design

4. squirrel design

6. Brum’s enormous tangled beard

14. painterly storybook 2D animation style

16. final basket-of-flowers cliffhanger

Animate the storyboard as one seamless cinematic short.

Do NOT animate all 15 storyboard panels as fifteen one-second hard cuts.

Combine the visual beats into approximately 8 coherent shots with natural cause → action → reaction storytelling.

--------------------------------------------------

[VIDEO GOAL]

Duration:
exactly 15 seconds

0:00–0:15

16:9 landscape

cinematic 24 fps

whimsical fantasy comedy

premium painterly 2D animated short film

family-friendly

English

native synchronized dialogue + character sounds + environment + SFX + music

EMOTIONAL ARC:

→ enormous sleepy reveal

→ silent teamwork
→ first flower success
→ dangerous BIG IDEA cliffhanger

Part 1 should feel cute, clever and increasingly mischievous.

--------------------------------------------------

[PART 1 STORY LIMIT]

PART 1 ONLY.

START:

Brum is peacefully asleep beneath the enormous ancient tree.

END:

Mimi has placed ONLY ONE flower in Brum’s beard.

She notices her still-full basket of flowers.

Her face lights up with a much bigger decorating idea.

Buzz realizes what she is thinking.

CUT TO BLACK.

DO NOT show:
- multiple flowers being added

- Brum’s nose twitch

- emotional approval

Those belong to PART 2.

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — MIMI]

Match the uploaded storyboard exactly.

MIMI is extremely tiny — approximately the height of one of Brum’s thumbs.

Appearance:

- soft rounded face
- fluffy chestnut-brown bob haircut

- natural loose strands around her face

Clothing:

- miniature florist pouch
- woven flower basket
- same clothing colors and proportions throughout

Personality:

- artistic
- curious
- highly confident
- caring
- easily inspired

PART 1 PERFORMANCE ARC:

0–3 sec:
happy forest traveler → startled by giant snore

3–5 sec:
concerned professional florist studying Brum’s beard

5–9 sec:
determined tiny adventurer

9–12 sec:
confident groomer → startled by squirrel

12–15 sec:
delighted by first flower → dangerous creative inspiration

Mimi must never look genuinely frightened of Brum.

She knows he is sleeping and believes she can improve his beard.

CRITICAL IDENTITY:

Maintain exact:

- clothing

- green outfit
- boots
- scale
- basket

No costume drift.

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — BRUM]

Match the storyboard exactly.

BRUM:

an enormous gentle mountain/forest giant.

Appearance:

- enormous rounded face
- giant broad nose
- thick dark-brown eyebrows
- huge fluffy chestnut/dark-brown beard
- very long tangled beard curls
- mossy green vest
- giant circular wooden buttons

Scale:

Brum must feel truly enormous.

Mimi:
approximately thumb-height.

close to Mimi’s torso height.

large enough for Mimi to stand beside and climb around.

Brum’s face:
many times larger than Mimi’s entire body.

Personality:

During PART 1 Brum NEVER fully wakes.

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — BUZZ]

PART 1 PERFORMANCE:

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — SQUIRREL]

The squirrel appears ONLY after Mimi removes a twig.

--------------------------------------------------

[BRUM'S BEARD — ENVIRONMENT LOCK]

Brum’s beard is not merely facial hair.

From Mimi’s scale, it behaves like a miniature landscape.

Use:
- enormous twisted beard curls

- tunnels between curls
- tangled twigs
- green leaves
- small moss patches
- tiny nest

The beard must still clearly look like natural hair.

Do not turn it into literal soil or forest ground.

Hair physics:
soft,
thick,
springy,
heavy relative to Mimi.

When Mimi brushes:
individual curls gently straighten and settle.

--------------------------------------------------

[KEY PROP LOCK — MIMI'S COMB]

Tiny turquoise/mint hand comb.

Small enough for Mimi.

Used to groom Brum’s beard carefully.

Do not change it into a full-sized human comb.

--------------------------------------------------

[KEY PROP LOCK — FLOWER BASKET]

Mimi’s woven florist basket.

Contains many colorful fresh flowers:
- pink
- purple
- yellow
- white
- small daisies
- little wildflowers
- leafy stems

At the beginning:
basket remains mostly full.

At the end:
only ONE flower has been removed.

The basket must still look obviously full.

This visual is essential to the cliffhanger.

--------------------------------------------------

[ENVIRONMENT LOCK — MAGICAL FOREST]

Maintain the exact storybook forest atmosphere from the uploaded storyboard.

Setting:
- enormous ancient tree
- thick trunk
- sprawling roots
- mossy forest floor
- ferns
- mushrooms
- wildflowers
- scattered leaves
- stones
- soft grassy patches
- distant forest depth
- warm golden sunlight filtering through canopy
- tiny drifting petals / leaves
- atmospheric dust motes

Brum sleeps at the base of the same tree.

Do not relocate him.

Lighting direction must remain coherent throughout.

--------------------------------------------------

[SCALE RULES]

Extreme scale contrast is one of the main visual hooks.

Always preserve:

Mimi = thumb-sized.

Buzz = approximately Mimi-head-sized.

Squirrel = comparable to / slightly larger than Mimi.

Brum = gigantic.

Important visual scale examples:

Mimi climbs one vest button like a stair.

Mimi walks through beard curls like thick tree roots.

One beard hair bundle is large enough for Mimi to hold.

Brum’s nose is enormous relative to Mimi.

Never accidentally render Mimi adult-human-sized next to Brum.

--------------------------------------------------

[WORLD / PHYSICS RULES]

All actions follow understandable physical cause and effect.

Brum snores
→ air pushes leaves and petals
→ Mimi and Buzz wobble backward.

Mimi sees messy beard
→ decides to fix it.

Mimi climbs giant vest
→ reaches beard.

Mimi combs beard
→ twig becomes loose.

Mimi pulls twig
→ squirrel hidden behind it appears.

Squirrel warns them
→ everyone becomes quiet.

Squirrel leaves
→ Mimi notices flower.

Mimi places ONE flower
→ beard looks unexpectedly beautiful.

Mimi sees full basket
→ inspiration escalates.

No unexplained magic.

The forest may feel magical, but no supernatural transformations occur in Part 1.

integrated_multimodal_description:

[SHOT 1] — 0.00s–1.80s
THE SLEEPING MOUNTAIN

Open with a beautiful cinematic wide shot of the enchanted forest clearing.

Golden sunlight streams through the huge tree canopy.

Tiny particles float in the light.

Wildflowers sway.

Camera slowly glides around one massive tree root.

Reveal BRUM asleep against the enormous ancient trunk.

His giant green-vested body occupies a large portion of the clearing.

His enormous brown beard spills across his chest like a tangled hillside.

Brum inhales.

Then—

<d>[English] SNORRRR...</d>

A harmless breath wave travels through nearby flowers.

Petals and leaves flutter outward.

Camera:
wide forest establishing shot
→ slow reveal of Brum
→ gentle push toward sleeping face.

Sound:
- forest birds
- leaves
- distant insects
- low peaceful giant breathing
- deep comedic snore
- flowers fluttering

Music:
soft magical flute + pizzicato + warm strings.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 2] — 1.80s–3.30s
THE TINY FLORIST MEETS THE SNORE

Cut much lower to Mimi’s scale.

Mimi walks into the clearing carrying her flower basket.

Buzz hovers beside her.

Another giant snore arrives.

A visible but soft gust pushes:
- Mimi’s short hair backward
- her yellow apron outward
- Buzz several centimeters sideways
- loose petals past camera

Mimi plants her tiny boots and leans forward against the breeze.

Buzz spins once and rights himself.

Mimi looks upward.

Her eyes grow huge when she finally understands the “hill” is a sleeping giant.

Dialogue:

Mimi:
<d>[English] Whoa...</d>

Buzz:
<d>[English] Very big napper.</d>

Mimi smiles rather than panics.

Camera:
low character-level medium shot
→ tilt upward along Brum’s enormous body
→ emphasize extreme scale.

Sound:
- snore gust
- cloth flutter
- Buzz wing buzz
- falling leaves

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 3] — 3.30s–5.00s
THE REAL PROBLEM

Mimi studies Brum.

Her attention moves from his peaceful sleeping face...

down into the enormous tangled beard.

Camera follows her gaze.

Reveal close beard details:

- tangled curls
- twig
- several leaves
- moss
- tiny nest tucked deep inside
- messy knots

Mimi puts one tiny hand to her chin like a professional stylist inspecting a client.

Her expression changes:

surprise
→ concern
→ determination.

Dialogue:

Mimi:
<d>[English] Buzz...</d>

Buzz:
<d>[English] Yes?</d>

Mimi points dramatically toward beard.

<d>[English] That beard needs help.</d>

Buzz stares at the enormous beard.

Then at sleeping Brum.

Buzz:
<d>[English] Of course it does.</d>

Camera:
Mimi close-up
→ POV into beard landscape
→ macro details
→ two-shot Mimi/Buzz.

Music:
small mischievous plucked motif begins.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 4] — 5.00s–6.80s
THE SECRET BEARD MISSION

Mimi tightens her florist pouch and starts climbing Brum’s moss-green vest.

She uses Brum’s gigantic wooden vest buttons like climbing steps.

Show scale clearly:

one button is nearly as large as Mimi’s torso.

Mimi climbs:
button
→ fold in vest
→ next button.

Buzz follows hovering close behind with her tiny supplies.

Brum continues breathing deeply.

His huge chest rises gently beneath them.

Mimi pauses for one breath as the “ground” rises.

Then continues.

Dialogue:

Mimi, whispering:
<d>[English] Quiet feet.</d>

Buzz:
<d>[English] You are climbing a giant.</d>

Mimi:
<d>[English] Very quiet feet.</d>

Camera:
side wide scale shot
→ low view upward along giant vest
→ close-up Mimi pulling herself onto final button
→ reveal beard looming above.

Sound:
- tiny boot taps
- fabric creak
- Buzz wing hum
- Brum breathing
- small button tap

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 5] — 6.80s–9.20s
THE BEARD LANDSCAPE

Mimi and Buzz reach Brum’s beard.

From their scale, enormous brown curls surround them like thick twisting vines.

Mimi steps carefully onto a broad curl.

She removes her tiny turquoise comb.

She begins gently combing one small section.

SCRITCH...
SCRITCH...

A leaf comes loose.

Then another.

One beard curl slowly becomes neat and smooth.

Brum’s sleeping mouth forms the tiniest peaceful smile.

Mimi notices.

Her confidence grows.

Dialogue:

Mimi, whispering:
<d>[English] See? He likes it.</d>

Buzz watches Brum nervously.

Buzz:
<d>[English] He is unconscious.</d>

Mimi:
<d>[English] Still counts.</d>

Camera:
wide beard-landscape reveal
→ close-up tiny comb moving through giant hair
→ leaf falling
→ cut to Brum’s sleepy smile
→ Mimi proud reaction.

Sound:
- soft comb through hair
- hair fibers
- leaves falling
- Brum sleepy exhale
- Buzz wing flutter

Music:
gentle playful grooming rhythm.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 6] — 9.20s–11.40s
THE SECRET RESIDENT

Mimi notices one stubborn twig lodged between beard curls.

She sets down the comb.

Grabs the twig with both tiny hands.

She pulls.

Once.

Nothing.

She braces one boot against a beard curl.

Pulls again.

The twig pops free.

Behind it—

two tiny eyes appear.

Mimi freezes.

Buzz freezes.

A warm-brown SQUIRREL suddenly pops out holding an acorn.

All three stare at each other.

One quiet beat.

Mimi:
<d>[English] Oh!</d>

Buzz:
<d>[English] Oh no.</d>

The squirrel puts one finger/paw near its mouth.

“Shhh.”

Then points upward toward sleeping Brum.

Mimi instantly understands.

She slowly puts a finger over her own lips.

Dialogue, whispered:

Mimi:
<d>[English] We won’t wake him.</d>

The squirrel gives a serious nod.

Camera:
close-up twig
→ tiny pull
→ reveal squirrel
→ reaction close-ups
→ wider four-layer scale composition with sleeping Brum behind.

Sound:
- twig creak
- tiny “pop”
- soft squirrel squeak
- music briefly stops for reveal
- Brum continuing to snore quietly

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 7] — 11.40s–13.30s
ONE LITTLE FLOWER

The squirrel quietly slips deeper through the beard and exits toward another curl, still carrying its acorn.

Mimi watches it go.

Then she notices a single small purple-pink wildflower caught/growing near the beard edge.

Her eyes brighten.

Buzz immediately recognizes the expression.

Buzz:
<d>[English] Mimi...</d>

Mimi gently plucks/picks up the loose flower.

Dialogue:

Mimi:
<d>[English] Just one.</d>

Buzz narrows his eyes skeptically.

Mimi carefully tucks the flower between two neat beard curls.

She adjusts it once.

Steps backward.

The tiny flower looks surprisingly beautiful against Brum’s huge brown beard.

A tiny warm sparkle of sunlight catches its petals — natural light, not magical transformation.

Mimi beams.

Dialogue:

Mimi:
<d>[English] Perfect.</d>

Camera:
flower insert
→ Mimi’s idea expression
→ gentle placement
→ close beauty shot of single flower
→ Mimi/Buzz reaction.

Sound:
- tiny petal rustle
- beard fibers
- soft musical “ting”

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 8] — 13.30s–15.00s
THE MUCH BIGGER IDEA — CLIFFHANGER

Mimi admires the ONE flower.

Then slowly turns.

Camera follows her eyes toward her woven basket.

The basket is overflowing with:

- pink flowers
- purple flowers
- white daisies
- yellow blossoms
- leafy stems

Mimi’s eyes widen.

Her mouth slowly curls into an enormous creative grin.

A metaphorical tiny “idea” sparkle may appear in her eye, but no literal floating lightbulb is necessary.

Buzz looks at Mimi.

Then the flower.

Then the enormous basket.

Then Brum’s gigantic beard.

Buzz’s expression becomes pure dread.

Buzz:
<d>[English] Mimi... no.</d>

Mimi slowly reaches toward the basket.

Mimi, delighted whisper:
<d>[English] Maybe... a few more?</d>

Buzz:
<d>[English] NO.</d>

Brum snores peacefully in the background.

The single flower gently trembles from his breath.

Camera:
close Mimi
→ POV basket
→ slow push into Mimi’s inspired grin
→ Buzz reaction
→ final wide shot showing tiny Mimi + full basket + enormous beard + sleeping Brum.

CUT TO BLACK.

END PART 1.

Do NOT begin decorating with additional flowers.

--------------------------------------------------

[AUDIO DESCRIPTION]

Generate synchronized native stereo audio.

--------------------------------------------------

[MIMI VOICE]

Natural young girl voice.

Qualities:

Her delivery should often become a whisper because Brum is asleep.

Animate the storyboard as one coherent cinematic sequence rather than 15 isolated panels.

--------------------------------------------------

[VIDEO GOAL]

IMPORTANT:

The uploaded storyboard contains 15 one-second panels.

Allow actions to flow naturally:

cause

PART 3 ONLY.

Luma sits beside a curled-up Nimbus and gently says:

[NIMBUS EMOTIONAL TRANSFORMATION]

STATE 2 — TRYING HARDER

STATE 4 — CURLED-UP NIMBUS

[LEAF RAFT LOCK]

[GREENHOUSE CONTINUITY LOCK]

--------------------------------------------------

[RAIN EFFECT SCALE]

LEVEL 4 around comedy peak:

The rain must never become:

--------------------------------------------------

[PHYSICS / STORY CAUSE-AND-EFFECT]

Clear logic:

Luma praised Nimbus
→ Nimbus believes she wants more.

Nimbus sees plants responding positively
→ he increases rain.

More rain
→ puddles form.

A large harmless splash lifts a broad leaf
→ Sprout accidentally rides it.

Luma continues giving encouraging signals
→ Nimbus pushes himself harder.

Nimbus uses too much energy
→ his rain weakens.

Luma notices his body language
→ her excitement disappears.

Nimbus attempts one final shower
→ cannot sustain it.

Nimbus runs out of energy
→ all rain stops.

Nimbus withdraws
→ Luma realizes he was trying to please her.

Luma stops asking for rain
→ sits beside Nimbus instead.

Emotional lesson begins.

integrated_multimodal_description:

[SHOT 1] — 0.00s–1.70s
THE EXPERIMENT IS WORKING TOO WELL

Continue directly from Part 2.

Open on a wide shot of the sunny-but-rainy rooftop greenhouse.

Nimbus floats in the center of the greenhouse.

He is:
- fluffy
- blue-white

- still cute and eager

Several localized rain showers fall beneath him.

SPLASH!

plant detail
→ Luma thumbs-up

→ gentle push-in on Nimbus preparing.

[SHOT 4] — 5.20s–7.40s
THE GREENHOUSE GETS ITS OWN WEATHER

then puffs outward.

More small showers appear.

Show an energetic continuous camera move through the greenhouse:

Sprout:
<d>[English] EEEP!</d>

- leaves

[SHOT 5] — 7.40s–8.70s

dizzy,

low close-up
→ gentle bump

[SHOT 6] — 8.70s–10.70s
LUMA SEES NIMBUS STRUGGLING

Cut to Nimbus.

Something is different.

His cloud motion is slower.

His smile weakens.

His edges begin drooping.

His eyes look heavy.

Raindrops underneath him become less regular.

Instead of smooth showers:

drip...

drip...

pause...

Nimbus tries to puff himself up again.

Luma stops running.

Nimbus:
<d>[English] I’m... helping.</d>

Camera:
close-up Nimbus
→ rack focus to Luma
→ intimate two-character composition.

Sound:
rain layers begin fading.

Music:
playful rhythm dissolves into a soft emotional pad/piano.

[SHOT 7] — 10.70s–12.20s
THE RAIN STOPS

Nimbus squeezes his eyes shut.

He tries one final time.

His cloud body puffs.

For one brief moment several droplets form.

Then—

the fluff collapses gently inward.

The droplets stop.

Every localized rain shower throughout the greenhouse ends almost simultaneously.

Important audio transition:

rain
→ fewer drops
→ one final drip
→ SILENCE.

Only droplets already hanging from leaves continue falling naturally.

PLINK.

PLINK.

Nimbus becomes visibly smaller.

His color shifts subtly:

bright blue-white
→ pale blue
→ muted soft blue-gray.

Luma stands completely still.

Sprout becomes quiet.

medium Nimbus
→ wide greenhouse shot as rain stops

No response.

Sound:
- rain fading

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 8] — 12.20s–13.50s
THE QUIET GREENHOUSE

Wide still composition.

No active rain.

Morning sunlight passes through wet greenhouse glass.

The floor contains small reflective puddles.

Luma stands quietly in the center.

Sprout has stepped off his leaf raft.

Both watch Nimbus.

Nimbus slowly drifts toward a quiet wooden shelf / planter corner surrounded by hanging vines.

His posture collapses.

He curls his cloud body inward.

The flower crown is crooked.

His eyes look down.

He settles into a compact sad little blue-gray cloud.

No dialogue for most of this shot.

almost nothing.

Camera:

→ stop at a respectful distance.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 9] — 13.50s–15.00s
“YOU DON’T HAVE TO RAIN FOR EVERYONE.”

Luma gently removes her bucket and sets it aside.

Do NOT have her immediately grab him.

Luma speaks softly:

Nimbus slowly raises his eyes toward her.

Luma:

<d>[English] You don’t have to rain for everyone.</d>

Nimbus blinks.

A SINGLE tiny raindrop begins forming beneath his cloud body.

This is NOT rain.

It is one tiny emotional magical drop.

A subtle rainbow glint catches inside it.

DO NOT let it fall.

Sprout watches quietly.

Camera:

eye-level two-shot Luma + Nimbus
→ subtle push-in
→ close emotional view
→ tiny raindrop forming at edge of frame.

No dialogue after Luma’s line.

Hold.

CUT TO BLACK.

END PART 3.

--------------------------------------------------

[VIDEO GOAL]

Duration: exactly 15 seconds

0:15–0:30 of the complete 1-minute film

16:9 landscape

cinematic 24 fps

premium painterly 2D animated film

whimsical magical garden comedy

BIG IDEA
→ excited experiment

→ Nimbus gets fluffier
→ first successful rain
→ Luma asks for more
→ Nimbus becomes more powerful

The sequence should feel increasingly energetic without ever becoming dangerous.

IMPORTANT:

The storyboard contains fifteen one-second panels.

cause

--------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — LUMA]

- loose curly strands framing face

- coral-and-cream striped shirt

- slightly oversized head
- rounded appealing silhouette
- small expressive hands

Personality:

- impulsively enthusiastic
- inventive
- convinced every successful idea should immediately become a bigger idea

PART 2 PERFORMANCE ARC:

thrilled
→ confident
→ playful
→ proud
→ increasingly amazed
→ briefly oblivious to warning signs
→ surprised
→ concerned at the final moment

Luma must genuinely believe she is helping Nimbus.

CRITICAL IDENTITY:

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — NIMBUS]

- rosy cheek tint

PART 2 GROWTH CONTINUITY:

Nimbus can gradually become approximately 20–35% larger and fluffier over the sequence.

Stage 1:

Stage 2:
flower-crown Nimbus.

Stage 3:

Stage 5:

DO NOT turn Nimbus into a giant cloud.

HAND FAN

WIND CHIMES

--------------------------------------------------

LEAF WATER CUP

[ENVIRONMENT LOCK]

--------------------------------------------------

[WEATHER / RAIN RULES]

Rain magic must remain:

cute,
localized,
gentle,
whimsical,
family-friendly.

Use approximately four visual rain levels.

LEVEL 1:

LEVEL 2:
one tiny plant-sized shower.

LEVEL 3:

LEVEL 4:

Never show:

Every increase must be motivated by Nimbus trying harder.

--------------------------------------------------

[CAUSE-AND-EFFECT LOGIC]

Luma believes Nimbus can help every flower.

Therefore:

Luma makes Nimbus feel special
→ Nimbus becomes confident.

Luma gently fans Nimbus
→ cloud fluff expands.

Luma adds pleasant wind chimes
→ Nimbus relaxes.

Luma gives Nimbus a tiny water refill
→ Nimbus becomes slightly fuller.

Nimbus waters one plant
→ plant responds positively.

Luma gets excited
→ asks him to try more.

Nimbus wants to make Luma happy
→ increases rain output.

Multiple plants receive rain
→ puddles appear.

Nimbus increases output again
→ greenhouse becomes busier.

Luma finally sees:
this may be too much.

The escalating rain comes from Nimbus’s eagerness, NOT from malfunction or anger.

integrated_multimodal_description:

[SHOT 1] — 0.00s–1.80s
LUMA’S BIG PLAN

Continue directly from Part 1.

Warm sunny greenhouse.

Luma stands excitedly near Nimbus.

Nimbus floats above the old watering can at his original tiny size.

Sprout sits nearby.

Luma spreads both arms wide toward the enormous greenhouse filled with flowers and plants.

Her expression says she has just discovered the greatest gardening solution ever.

Nimbus looks from Luma...

to all the plants...

then back to Luma.

OPERATION SUPER CLOUD

<d>[English] Operation Super Cloud!</d>

Nimbus:
<d>[English] Super... Cloud?</d>

Luma rapidly but safely gathers a few harmless gardening items.

She returns with the supplies in a gardening basket.

→ Sprout suspicious reaction.

[SHOT 3] — 3.60s–5.50s
CLOUD STYLE + EXTRA FLUFF

Luma kneels beside floating Nimbus.

First she carefully places the tiny flower crown over the top of his fluffy body.

Nimbus blushes.

His cloud cheeks turn slightly rosy.

Luma:

<d>[English] Perfect!</d>

Nimbus gives a shy smile.

Then Luma lifts the small floral hand fan.

She gently fans him.

WHOOSH...

Not strong.

Just a pleasant garden breeze.

Nimbus’s cloud edges flutter.

He gradually expands slightly.

His silhouette becomes fluffier and rounder.

His little raindrop feet sway.

Nimbus looks down at himself in surprise.

Dialogue:

<d>[English] Now... extra fluff!</d>

Nimbus:
<d>[English] Oh!</d>

Luma laughs softly.

Sprout looks from the fan to Nimbus.

Sprout’s expression:
“This cannot possibly end well.”

→ side angle showing fan breeze

→ Sprout deadpan reaction.

- fan flutter

[SHOT 4] — 5.50s–7.30s
PREMIUM WEATHER ATMOSPHERE + RAIN REFILL

Luma hangs the little wind chimes beside Nimbus.

They gently sway in the greenhouse breeze.

TING...
TING...

Nimbus closes his eyes.

He relaxes and floats with the rhythm.

His cloud edges ripple softly.

Luma then holds out the green leaf cup containing several sparkling droplets.

Dialogue:

<d>[English] And one tiny refill.</d>

Nimbus opens his eyes.

Nimbus:
<d>[English] For me?</d>

Luma:
<d>[English] For the Super Cloud.</d>

Nimbus shyly leans toward the leaf.

He absorbs/sips the water.

The water disappears gently into his cloud body.

A subtle pale-blue ripple travels through his fluff.

He becomes slightly larger.

One or two droplets begin hanging beneath him.

Sprout notices.

His eyes widen.

Camera:
wind-chime close-up
→ Nimbus relaxing
→ leaf-cup insert
→ close-up water absorbing into cloud
→ slight push-out showing larger Nimbus
→ Sprout reaction.

- soft magical puff
- Sprout nervous squeak

[SHOT 5] — 7.30s–9.30s
THE FIRST TEST

Luma guides Nimbus gently toward one small potted plant.

Nimbus positions himself approximately 30–40 cm above the plant.

He looks nervous.

Luma kneels nearby with hands clasped.

Sprout watches from behind the pot.

Luma whispers:

Nimbus nods.

He closes his eyes.

His body compresses slightly.

A tiny localized drizzle begins.

Approximately six to ten small droplets fall onto the plant.

The soil darkens.

Leaves lift gently.

A tiny bud opens.

Nimbus opens his eyes.

Luma gasps happily.

Nimbus:
<d>[English] It did?</d>

Luma:

- tiny magical chime

She points.

Nimbus looks at the plants.

Then proudly nods.

Nimbus:
<d>[English] I can try!</d>

Nimbus puffs himself slightly bigger.

A second localized drizzle begins.

Then another.

Now two or three nearby plants receive gentle rain simultaneously.

Luma moves between them, amazed.

Sprout slowly backs away.

Camera:
medium Luma pointing
→ close-up Nimbus determined face
→ pull wider as multiple little showers appear
→ Sprout slowly retreating.

- multiple gentle rain layers
- plant leaves moving
- growing whimsical score

Luma:
<d>[English] Nimbus, you’re amazing!</d>

Nimbus beams.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 7] — 11.20s–13.20s
THE GARDEN GETS MORE RAIN THAN IT ORDERED

Nimbus hears Luma’s praise.

He wants to help even more.

His eyes brighten.

Nimbus:

<d>[English] More?</d>

Before Luma fully answers, Nimbus takes a deeper breath.

His cloud body becomes puffier.

Several gentle showers begin across different nearby pots.

Important:
keep showers localized, soft and visually charming.

Rain droplets:
- bounce off leaves
- sparkle in sunlight
- create tiny shallow puddles
- run down terracotta pots
- form small rainbow reflections

One empty lightweight gardening pot near the floor becomes wet and slowly tips onto its side.

No plant is damaged.

Sprout gets splashed by one large harmless droplet.

His eyes become enormous.

Sprout:
“EEP!”

Luma initially laughs.

<d>[English] Wow!</d>

She looks around.

Her smile slowly changes.

Luma:
<d>[English] Wait...</d>

→ close splash on Sprout
→ tipping empty pot
→ Luma realization.

tempo increases into cheerful escalating comedy.

Sound:

- little splashes
- leaves

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 8] — 13.20s–15.00s
THE SUPER CLOUD IDEA GETS VERY SUPER

Wide greenhouse shot.

Nimbus now floats higher in the center.

He is still cute and cheerful.

He is approximately 25–35% fluffier than at Part 1.

His flower crown remains visible.

Multiple gentle plant-sized rain showers fall beneath and around him.

Show:
- wet leaves
- tiny puddles
- dripping planters
- colorful flowers
- glistening greenhouse floor

Luma moves beneath Nimbus.

At first:
delighted.

Then a shower begins beside her.

Another begins behind her.

She turns.

Another plant starts receiving rain.

Her eyes widen.

She lifts both hands.

Dialogue:

<d>[English] Nimbus?</d>

Nimbus happily:
<d>[English] I’m helping!</d>

Luma:

Nimbus does not fully hear/understand yet.

He continues gently raining.

Sprout appears beside Luma.

Completely soaked.

He looks directly toward Luma with maximum comic disbelief.

Luma looks at Sprout.

Then looks at Nimbus.

Final line:

<d>[English] Uh-oh.</d>

Sprout gives a dramatic:

Camera:
large wide composition
→ slow push toward Luma
→ Nimbus above
→ Sprout reaction
→ end on three-character composition.

FINAL STORY EMOTIONS:

happy and eager.

Luma:
finally worried.

Sprout:
full dramatic panic.

CUT TO BLACK.

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — [REFERENCE CONTROL] Use the uploaded PART 1 storyboard image for “The Little Gardener and the Runaway Rain Cloud” as the PRIMARY visual, character, environment, composition, and...</summary>

~~~~text
[REFERENCE CONTROL]

Use the uploaded PART 1 storyboard image for “The Little Gardener and the Runaway Rain Cloud” as the PRIMARY visual, character, environment, composition, and narrative reference.

Generate a FINISHED animated sequence from the storyboard.

DO NOT reproduce the storyboard sheet itself.

The final video must NOT contain:
- storyboard borders
- panel numbers
- panel captions
- timestamps
- production notes
- character reference sheets
- off-white storyboard paper
- storyboard title/header
- handwritten annotations

The uploaded storyboard controls and locks:

1. Luma’s exact character design
2. Nimbus’s exact cloud design
3. Sprout’s exact snail design
4. the rooftop greenhouse architecture
5. the wilted sunflower design
6. the old metal watering can
7. the gardening props
8. warm watercolor/painterly 2D visual style
9. character scale relationships
10. overall color palette
11. Part 1 story progression

Animate these designs naturally while maintaining extremely high identity consistency.

--------------------------------------------------

[VIDEO GOAL]

Create exactly:

Duration: 15 seconds
Aspect Ratio: 16:9 landscape
Frame rate feeling: cinematic 24 fps
Genre: magical garden comedy / heartwarming fantasy
Visual medium: premium hand-painted 2D animated short
Audience: family-friendly
Dialogue language: English
Native synchronized dialogue + sound effects + music

Emotional progression:

peaceful garden morning
→ concern
→ determination
→ mysterious sound
→ magical discovery
→ tiny comic surprise
→ hopeful miracle
→ BIG IDEA cliffhanger

The storytelling must be very easy to understand even without narration.

IMPORTANT:

Although the storyboard contains 15 individual panels, DO NOT turn the video into fifteen frantic one-second cuts.

Consolidate those beats into approximately 7–8 fluid cinematic shots.

Each shot should contain several connected storyboard actions so the animation feels like an actual polished animated short rather than a slideshow.

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — LUMA]

LUMA must match the uploaded storyboard exactly.

Character:
approximately 8-year-old little gardener.

Physical appearance:
- warm fair skin
- large expressive dark brown eyes
- soft round childlike face
- freckles across cheeks/nose if visible in reference
- fluffy auburn / reddish-brown hair
- hair gathered into two messy buns
- several loose curly strands around face

Signature clothing:
- oversized warm yellow gardening hat
- mint-green gardening overalls
- coral-and-cream horizontally striped shirt
- red gardening boots
- tiny brown gardening/seed pouch attached at waist

Character proportions:
- small child stature
- slightly oversized head
- expressive hands
- rounded appealing silhouette

Personality:
- caring
- cheerful
- imaginative
- curious
- determined when a plant needs help
- easily excited by magical discoveries

Performance should transition:

happy gardener
→ worried plant caretaker
→ determined problem solver
→ curious investigator
→ amazed child
→ excited inventor

CRITICAL:

Luma must remain visually identical in every shot.

Do not change:
- face
- age
- hairstyle
- hair color
- hat
- clothing
- boots
- proportions
- eye color

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — NIMBUS]

Nimbus must match the cloud discovered inside the watering can in the storyboard.

Nimbus is a tiny living rain cloud approximately pillow-sized.

Design:
- soft fluffy blue-white cloud body
- subtle watercolor edges
- very faint pale blue shadows
- large round expressive dark eyes
- tiny eyebrows
- small cute mouth
- slight rosy blush
- tiny dangling raindrop-shaped feet
- soft semi-translucent cloud fluff
- no arms unless naturally suggested by cloud shape

Personality:
- shy
- timid
- gentle
- easily embarrassed
- harmless
- sweet

Nimbus begins PART 1 hiding inside the watering can.

He must NOT appear in the greenhouse before the discovery sequence.

His first reveal should feel magical and surprising.

Nimbus should remain very small.

DO NOT:
- enlarge Nimbus
- darken Nimbus
- create storm powers
- add flower crown yet
- make him Super Cloud
- generate multiple Nimbus clouds
- create lightning

Those belong to later story beats.

--------------------------------------------------

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK — SPROUT]

Sprout must match the storyboard exactly.

Design:
- tiny green garden snail
- soft rounded body
- large tall expressive eye stalks
- warm brown spiral shell
- tiny yellow flower growing beside/on shell
- same miniature scale throughout

Personality:
- slow
- observant
- dramatic
- suspicious
- comedic reaction character

Sprout has no human speech unless specified.

Use:
- tiny squeaks
- gasps
- nervous chirps
- eye-stalk reactions

Sprout should frequently understand the danger of Luma’s ideas before Luma does.

--------------------------------------------------

[PROP IDENTITY LOCK — WILTED SUNFLOWER]

Use the exact sunflower from the storyboard.

At the start:
- heavily drooping yellow petals
- bowed flower head
- wilted leaves
- weak stem
- planted in rectangular wooden planter
- still alive

After ONE drop from Nimbus:
the flower should perk up slightly.

IMPORTANT:

Do NOT magically transform it into a giant perfect sunflower yet.

Only show:
- stem becoming slightly stronger
- leaves lifting
- flower head rising a little
- color becoming slightly healthier

This is a small visual miracle.

--------------------------------------------------

[PROP IDENTITY LOCK — WATERING CAN]

Use the same old watering can from the storyboard.

Appearance:
- weathered gray/blue metal
- rounded main body
- curved metal handle
- long narrow spout
- slightly worn surface
- small painted flower decoration if visible
- large enough for tiny Nimbus to curl inside

It is an ordinary gardening can.

Do not transform it into a magical object.

Nimbus is simply hiding inside.

--------------------------------------------------

[ENVIRONMENT LOCK — ROOFTOP GREENHOUSE]

Maintain exactly the warm rooftop greenhouse established by the storyboard.

Environment:
- glass-paneled greenhouse walls
- glass roof
- wooden structural framing
- warm sunlight pouring through glass
- hanging vines
- multiple wooden planter boxes
- terracotta flower pots
- colorful flowers
- herbs
- leafy plants
- gardening shelves
- small ladder
- watering hose
- gardening tools
- seed packets
- old watering can
- pots and wooden crates
- distant soft city rooftop skyline through greenhouse glass

Visual feeling:
handmade,
lived-in,
cozy,
safe,
sunny,
lush,
magical without obvious fantasy effects at the beginning.

Maintain coherent geography throughout.

The sunflower planter, watering can area, shelves, and main walkway must not randomly move between shots.

--------------------------------------------------

[WORLD / MAGIC RULES]

The world begins mostly realistic.

Nimbus introduces the first visible magical element.

Magic should be:
- delicate
- soft
- believable within a children’s animated world
- visually elegant
- never explosive

Nimbus creates rain naturally.

Cause-and-effect must be clear:

sunflower is dry
→ Luma searches for water
→ watering can sneezes
→ Luma investigates
→ Nimbus is discovered
→ Nimbus nervously sneezes
→ one raindrop falls
→ sunflower responds
→ Luma realizes Nimbus can help
→ Luma gets a BIG IDEA

No unexplained magical events.

--------------------------------------------------

integrated_multimodal_description:

[SHOT 1] (0.00s–2.00s)
THE PEACEFUL ROOFTOP GARDEN

Open with a beautiful wide establishing shot of the rooftop greenhouse.

Warm morning sunlight enters through the glass ceiling.

Tiny dust particles float through the golden beams.

Leaves move gently in a rooftop breeze.

Luma happily walks between planter boxes carrying a small basket of seeds/gardening tools.

Sprout slowly crawls along the edge of a wooden planter beside her.

Use subtle environmental life:
- small leaves swaying
- hanging vines moving
- distant city haze
- butterflies or tiny garden insects only if subtle

Camera:

begin outside/near the glass roof
→ smooth gentle push through the greenhouse
→ settle into a medium-wide shot of Luma tending plants.

Luma hums quietly.

She pats soil around a healthy flower.

Dialogue:

Luma, cheerful:
<d>[English] Good morning, little guys!</d>

She notices Sprout.

Luma:
<d>[English] Morning, Sprout.</d>

Sprout gives a tiny cheerful squeak.

Sound:
- light morning breeze
- gentle birds outside
- leaves rustling
- gardening basket movement
- soft snail squeak
- quiet greenhouse ambience

Music:
delicate whimsical opening using pizzicato strings, gentle marimba, and soft flute.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 2] (2.00s–4.20s)
THE WILTING SUNFLOWER

As Luma continues through the garden, her smile slowly fades.

Her gaze lands on the drooping sunflower.

Cut to a closer view:

the sunflower’s head hangs sadly downward.

Its leaves droop.

Sprout crawls beside the planter and slowly raises both eye stalks toward the flower.

One eye stalk leans sideways with concern.

Luma immediately kneels.

She gently lifts one wilted leaf between her fingers.

Camera:

medium tracking with Luma
→ rack focus from healthy flowers to wilted sunflower
→ close-up flower
→ intimate medium-close shot of Luma kneeling.

Dialogue:

Luma:
<d>[English] Oh no...</d>

She gently examines the plant.

Luma:
<d>[English] Sunny, what happened?</d>

She touches the dry soil.

Luma:
<d>[English] You’re thirsty.</d>

Sprout gives a worried tiny squeak.

Luma’s worried expression becomes determined.

Luma:
<d>[English] Don’t worry. I’ll find water.</d>

Sound:
- soft soil touch
- leaf rustle
- subtle worried musical note

Keep this emotionally sincere but brief.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 3] (4.20s–6.30s)
THE SEARCH

Luma stands quickly and begins checking the greenhouse.

She looks at:
- empty small watering cup
- dry watering station
- hose
- gardening shelf

She trots along the walkway.

Sprout follows at an absurdly slow speed.

Use a brief visual comedy beat:

Luma rushes past frame.

A moment later...

Sprout is still only a few centimeters farther along.

Luma then notices the old metal watering can sitting among leafy pots.

Her eyes brighten.

Camera:

side tracking shot following Luma
→ small comedic static shot of slow Sprout
→ quick pan toward watering can as Luma notices it.

Dialogue:

Luma:
<d>[English] Water... water...</d>

She spots the can.

Luma:
<d>[English] Aha!</d>

Sound:
- light footsteps
- tool clinks
- leaves brushing clothes
- tiny Sprout effort squeak

Music becomes slightly more playful.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 4] (6.30s–8.30s)
THE WATERING CAN SNEEZES

Luma approaches the old watering can.

Before she reaches it—

something inside shifts.

Very subtle.

Luma freezes.

The watering can gives a tiny wobble.

Then:

<d>[English] Ah... ah...</d>

Luma slowly leans closer.

Sprout’s eye stalks rise behind her.

Suddenly from inside:

<d>[English] ACHOO!</d>

A tiny puff of cool mist shoots gently from the watering-can spout.

The can rocks slightly.

Luma jumps backward only a few centimeters in surprise.

Sprout instantly retracts slightly into his shell.

No danger.

Camera:

slow push-in on watering can
→ close-up of its dark opening
→ Luma listening
→ mist puff from spout
→ reaction two-shot.

Dialogue:

Luma:
<d>[English] ...Did you just sneeze?</d>

Beat.

She looks toward Sprout.

Luma:
<d>[English] Watering cans don’t sneeze.</d>

Sprout gives a tiny uncertain squeak.

Sound:
- metal can creak
- tiny movement from inside
- adorable sneeze
- mist “pfft”
- light metallic wobble

Music:
brief curious plucked-string motif.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 5] (8.30s–10.30s)
THE IMPOSSIBLE DISCOVERY

Luma carefully crouches beside the watering can.

She grips its edge gently.

Slowly...

she peers inside.

Camera moves over Luma’s shoulder toward the dark interior.

For one beat we see only darkness.

Then two tiny eyes blink.

Soft pale-blue cloud fluff emerges.

Nimbus is curled inside the watering can.

He looks up at Luma.

Nimbus immediately becomes embarrassed and tries to shrink deeper into the can.

Luma’s expression transforms from caution to pure wonder.

Sprout stretches upward from beside the can to see inside.

Camera:

over-shoulder POV
→ gradual focus transition revealing Nimbus
→ close-up Nimbus eyes
→ reaction close-up Luma
→ wider three-character composition.

Dialogue:

Luma, whispering:
<d>[English] Whoa...</d>

Nimbus nervously:
<d>[English] ...Hi.</d>

Luma:
<d>[English] You’re a cloud.</d>

Nimbus gives a shy tiny nod.

Luma, amazed:
<d>[English] A tiny cloud!</d>

Sound:
- subtle magical shimmer
- tiny cloud puff
- soft metal resonance from inside can
- Nimbus’s faint watery ambience

Music:
introduce delicate glockenspiel and airy flute.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 6] (10.30s–12.20s)
THE TINY RAIN DROP

Nimbus slowly floats just above the watering-can opening.

He is nervous about being watched.

Luma leans closer with fascinated eyes.

Sprout watches from below.

Nimbus’s little face scrunches.

His cloud body briefly compresses.

Nimbus:

<d>[English] Ah... ah...</d>

Luma realizes another sneeze is coming.

Nimbus:

<d>[English] Achoo!</d>

A single sparkling raindrop pops from the underside of Nimbus.

IMPORTANT:

Only ONE meaningful raindrop.

Camera follows the droplet in a graceful macro tracking shot.

The droplet catches sunlight and briefly shows a subtle rainbow refraction.

It falls toward the sunflower planter.

Sound:
- cute cloud sneeze
- tiny watery “plink”
- soft magical shimmer

Luma:
<d>[English] Wait...!</d>

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 7] (12.20s–13.50s)
ONE DROP IS ENOUGH

Extreme close-up:

the raindrop lands in the dry soil near the sunflower stem.

A soft ring of moisture spreads through the soil.

Nothing explosive.

The sunflower responds gently.

Its stem firms.

One leaf slowly lifts.

The flower head rises a few centimeters.

Its yellow petals regain a little warmth.

Luma watches from the background.

Her mouth falls open.

Then she clasps her hands with delighted excitement.

Nimbus looks surprised by what he just did.

Sprout’s eyes become enormous.

Camera:

macro raindrop impact
→ tilt upward following sunflower
→ rack focus to Luma’s astonished face.

Dialogue:

Luma:
<d>[English] You did that?</d>

Nimbus, shy:
<d>[English] I... think so.</d>

Luma:
<d>[English] One drop!</d>

Sound:
- small water impact
- subtle plant movement
- soft magical chime
- leaf unfurl sound

Music:
warm hopeful melodic lift.

--------------------------------------------------

[SHOT 8] (13.50s–15.00s)
THE BIG IDEA — CLIFFHANGER

Luma looks from the revived sunflower...

to Nimbus...

then slowly around at the dozens of plants filling the greenhouse.

Her eyes widen.

A playful “idea” expression spreads across her face.

She turns toward Nimbus with a huge excited smile and raises one finger.

Nimbus immediately becomes slightly nervous.

Sprout looks at Luma.

Then at Nimbus.

Then at the enormous greenhouse.

Sprout’s eye stalks shoot upward in comic alarm.

Luma excitedly gestures toward all the plants.

Dialogue:

Luma:
<d>[English] Nimbus...</d>

Tiny pause.

Luma:
<d>[English] You can help ALL my flowers!</d>

Nimbus:
<d>[English] ...All?</d>

Sprout gives a loud alarmed:

<d>[English] Eep!</d>

Hold briefly on the three expressions:

Luma = thrilled

Nimbus = shy uncertainty

Sprout = “this is going to be a disaster”

Camera:

medium shot behind Luma looking across greenhouse
→ quick close-up on Luma’s excited realization
→ reaction close-up Nimbus
→ reaction close-up Sprout
→ final wide three-character composition.

End with a gentle comedic musical sting.

CUT TO BLACK.

DO NOT show Luma upgrading Nimbus.

DO NOT begin Part 2.

--------------------------------------------------

[AUDIO DESCRIPTION]

Generate synchronized native stereo audio.

LUMA VOICE:

Natural young girl voice, approximately 8 years old.

Voice qualities:
- warm
- bright
- playful
- emotionally expressive
- curious
- not overly cartoonish
- clear natural English

Her voice progression:

0–4 sec:
gentle and caring

4–8 sec:
determined then curious

8–12 sec:
soft wonder

12–15 sec:
high-energy excitement

--------------------------------------------------

NIMBUS VOICE:

Very soft youthful cloud voice.

Characteristics:
- shy
- slightly breathy
- adorable
- gentle
- innocent
- quiet
- slightly higher pitched than Luma but easily understandable

Do NOT make Nimbus sound robotic.

His sneezes should be small and cute.

--------------------------------------------------

SPROUT AUDIO:

No full normal dialogue.

Use:
- tiny squeaks
- nervous chirps
- tiny gasps
- comic “eep”

Do not give Sprout a human adult voice.

--------------------------------------------------

[ENVIRONMENT AUDIO]

Maintain a rich but soft greenhouse sound bed:

- distant rooftop birds
- very light city ambience far below
- glass greenhouse creaks
- subtle wind
- leaves rustling
- hanging plants moving
- gardening tools
- footsteps on stone/wood
- soil movement
- watering-can metal noises
- small water droplets
- Nimbus cloud puffs
- Sprout squeaks

Never overpower dialogue.

--------------------------------------------------

[NON-DIEGETIC MUSIC]

Create an original whimsical family-animation score.

Instrumentation:
- gentle pizzicato strings
- marimba
- soft acoustic plucks
- airy flute
- glockenspiel
- very subtle warm pads

Musical arc:

0.00–2.00
peaceful garden theme.

2.00–4.20
slightly concerned but still warm.

4.20–6.30
playful search rhythm.

6.30–8.30
curious comedic suspense.

8.30–10.30
magical discovery.

10.30–13.50
wonder and hopeful warmth.

13.50–15.00
playful “big idea” motif ending on light comedic suspense.

No epic music.
No scary music.
No dramatic disaster music.

--------------------------------------------------

[CAMERA LANGUAGE]

Use cinematic storybook camera work.

Overall camera rhythm:

Beginning:
wide, calm, observational.

Problem:
closer emotional coverage.

Mystery:
slow push-ins.

Discovery:
intimate close-ups.

Magic:
macro insert + graceful tracking.

Final idea:
reaction-driven comedy framing.

Preferred techniques:
- gentle dolly
- subtle parallax
- slow push-in
- rack focus
- over-the-shoulder composition
- macro raindrop shot
- reaction close-ups
- wide environmental establishing shot

Avoid:
- handheld shake
- crash zoom
- extreme Dutch angles
- fast action-camera motion
- excessive cuts
- 360-degree spins
- random camera movement

--------------------------------------------------

[VISUAL STYLE]

Premium finished painterly 2D animated short.

Match the storyboard’s exact aesthetic:

- delicate ink outlines
- watercolor-like painted fills
- warm children’s-book texture
- cinematic lighting
- painterly background detail
- softly textured paper-like brushwork without showing actual paper
- appealing rounded characters
- expressive facial animation
- subtle squash and stretch
- natural secondary hair/clothing motion
- charming hand-crafted environment

The final video should look like an animated film version of the storyboard artwork.

NOT:
- 3D CGI
- photorealism
- anime
- flat vector art
- cheap motion comic
- slideshow
- storyboard animation

--------------------------------------------------

[COLOR / LIGHTING]

Primary palette:

Warm golden sunlight
+
fresh leafy green
+
mint green
+
sunflower yellow
+
coral orange
+
soft sky blue

Luma:
warm sunlight on face and hair.

Nimbus:
cool pale-blue-white color providing subtle visual contrast.

Greenhouse:
bright, airy, naturally saturated.

Sunlight should create:
- leaf-shaped shadows
- soft rim lights
- reflections through glass
- golden dust particles
- subtle bloom

During Nimbus reveal:
slightly cooler soft blue fill from the watering can interior.

During raindrop:
tiny prism/rainbow highlight.

Do NOT darken the greenhouse dramatically.

--------------------------------------------------

[ANIMATION / MOTION RULES]

LUMA:

Use believable child motion.

Examples:
- small energetic steps
- expressive hands
- head tilts
- natural blinking
- loose auburn hair bouncing
- hat shifting subtly as she runs
- clothing following body movement

Avoid:
- rubber limbs
- extreme cartoon deformation
- unnatural running

NIMBUS:

Motion should feel buoyant.

Use:
- subtle floating
- soft vertical bob
- cloud edges gently expanding/contracting
- tiny squash when sneezing
- raindrop feet swaying
- slight drifting inertia

SPROUT:

Movement should be very slow.

Comedy comes from contrast with Luma.

Use:
- eye-stalk stretching
- tiny shell shifts
- slow sliding
- sudden eye reactions

PLANTS:

Use gentle natural motion:
- leaves bending
- flower head lifting
- vines swaying
- tiny response after water

--------------------------------------------------

[CONTINUITY RULES]

Maintain one consistent Luma.

Maintain one consistent Nimbus.

Maintain one consistent Sprout.

Do not duplicate characters.

The sunflower remains the same flower throughout.

The watering can remains spatially consistent.

Luma’s seed pouch remains visible.

Luma’s yellow hat remains on her head during Part 1.

Nimbus remains tiny throughout Part 1.

Sprout remains significantly smaller than Luma and Nimbus.

The greenhouse layout must remain coherent between shots.

Morning lighting direction must remain consistent.

--------------------------------------------------

[STORY RESTRICTIONS — PART 1 ONLY]

DO NOT show:

- Super Cloud mode
- flower crown on Nimbus
- hand fan upgrade
- wind chimes upgrade
- Nimbus drinking water
- Nimbus growing larger
- multiple rain showers
- greenhouse rain chaos
- puddle rafting
- overwhelmed Nimbus
- gray Nimbus
- magical greenhouse transformation
- rainbow-drop charm
- final ending

These belong to later parts.

Part 1 ends ONLY when Luma realizes Nimbus could help all her flowers.

--------------------------------------------------

[NEGATIVE CONSTRAINTS]

No storyboard borders.
No storyboard panels.
No captions.
No subtitles.
No timestamps.
No production notes.
No title card.
No watermark.
No brand logo.

No character identity drift.
No extra children.
No extra Nimbus clouds.
No duplicate Sprout.
No hairstyle changes.
No costume changes.
No missing hat.
No changing seed pouch.
No adult-looking Luma.

No dark storm.
No scary cloud.
No evil eyes.
No thunder.
No lightning.
No flooding.
No destructive rain.
No windstorm.
No danger.
No injury.
No broken plants.
No horror.

No random magical objects.
No visual teleportation.
No background redesign.

No excessive camera movement.
No frame interpolation artifacts.
No warped hands.
No extra fingers.
No malformed faces.
No melting character anatomy.
No inconsistent eye placement.

--------------------------------------------------

[FINAL 1.5-SECOND PRIORITY]

The ending must have crystal-clear storytelling.

Luma looks across the entire greenhouse.

She realizes Nimbus might be able to water every plant.

Her face fills with enormous excitement.

She turns to him and says:

<d>[English] Nimbus... you can help ALL my flowers!</d>

Nimbus becomes uncertain:

<d>[English] ...All?</d>

Sprout looks toward the huge greenhouse and reacts:

<d>[English] Eep!</d>

Final emotional composition:

Luma:
excited and confident.

Nimbus:
tiny, shy, slightly worried.

Sprout:
comic dread.

Warm beautiful greenhouse behind them.

Hold long enough for all three expressions to register.

Then:

CUT TO BLACK.

This cut should make the viewer immediately want to watch PART 2.
~~~~

{
  "title": "Wake the Wonder",
