~~~~text
  "prompt": "Create a fast, imaginative 15-second vertical animated advertisement about escaping routine through curiosity. Open immediately inside a silent library located on a tiny moon. A middle-aged lunar librarian wearing a bubble helmet and neat retro uniform sits behind a circular desk, mechanically stamping identical grey books. Endless shelves curve around the moon's interior while dusty speech bubbles reading SAME OLD, ALREADY KNOW, WHY BOTHER and NOTHING NEW drift through the air. The librarian's movements are repetitive and perfectly synchronized with a ticking clock. At second 3.0, a small coral-colored book falls from the highest shelf and lands open on the desk. Instead of printed words, a miniature galaxy spins between its pages. The librarian's eyes widen. They slam the date stamp onto the glowing page, sending a teal shockwave through the library. Shelves bend into planetary rings, book pages become flocks of geometric spacecraft and the dusty speech bubbles burst into constellations. The librarian leaps onto a giant flying page and surfs through the transforming archive while the camera spirals beside them. Every book they pass opens into a different illustrated world: an underwater city, a jungle growing on an asteroid, a mechanical whale and a floating orchestra. At the climax, the librarian flies toward a locked circular door labelled NOTHING NEW. The glowing book opens in front of it and transforms the door into a vast cosmic iris. They burst through into an infinite colorful universe made of pages, stars and painted shapes. Finish with the librarian floating confidently above the small moon while bold retro typography appears: OPEN ONE DOOR. EXPAND YOUR WORLD. Maintain rapid wonder, clear visual storytelling, elegant graphic design and continuous transformation.",
~~~~

## 29. Storyboard 2 to Video using MiniMax H3 on

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/storyboard-2-to-video-using-minimax-h3-on-852170/video-6383c31206b6.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/storyboard-2-to-video-using-minimax-h3-on-852170/poster-9b2051a10043.jpg" alt="Storyboard 2 to Video using MiniMax H3 on video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — [REFERENCE CONTROL] Use the uploaded PART 2 storyboard image as the PRIMARY visual, character, prop, environment, camera, lighting, and narrative reference for the entire video....</summary>

~~~~text
[REFERENCE CONTROL]

Use the uploaded PART 2 storyboard image as the PRIMARY visual, character, prop, environment, camera, lighting, and narrative reference for the entire video.

Use the storyboard to preserve:
- exact Part 2 story progression
- Lena Cruz and Jax Mercer identity continuity from Part 1
- Rico Vale’s appearance and attitude
- the dark muscle car
- black SUV convoy
- black drive
- unfinished overpass environment
- sunset-to-night lighting progression
- graphic-novel cinematic style
- chase escalation
- standoff
- Jax’s “disabled drive” reveal
- Lena and Jax recovering the drive
- final victorious walk-away

Do NOT reproduce the storyboard sheet itself.

The finished video must contain:
- no panel borders
- no storyboard numbers
- no timestamps
- no captions
- no production notes
- no paper background
- no reference-sheet layout

Convert the entire board into one coherent finished cinematic animated sequence.

[VIDEO GOAL]

Duration: exactly 15 seconds
Aspect ratio: 16:9 landscape
Video style: premium cinematic 2D graphic-novel animation
Genre: Hollywood-style action thriller / road chase
Tone: fast, stylish, tense, controlled, cinematic, triumphant

Narrative progression:

high-speed pursuit
→ convoy closes in
→ Jax breaks formation
→ unfinished overpass
→ convoy collapses
→ Rico standoff
→ disabled-drive twist
→ brief safe disarm
→ drive recovered
→ cool final exit

This is PART 2 only.

Begin immediately with the chase already underway.

Do NOT repeat the gas-station setup from Part 1.

End exactly after Lena and Jax recover the black drive and walk toward their muscle car while Jax delivers the final line:

“Enough to burn his empire.”

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCKS]

LENA CRUZ

Young athletic woman.

Appearance:
- warm medium skin
- sharp expressive eyes
- high dark ponytail
- red patterned bandana over lower face or around neck
- fitted dark cropped top
- light denim shorts
- practical dark footwear
- lean athletic proportions
- confident silhouette

Personality:
bold, reactive, fearless, intuitive, aggressive under pressure but controlled.

Beginning emotion:
focused pursuit intensity.

Ending emotion:
quiet satisfaction and confidence.

Movement:
fast head turns,
athletic body control,
decisive reactions,
strong braced posture during the car chase,
quick efficient movements during confrontation.

Keep identical in every shot:
- face
- skin tone
- ponytail
- bandana
- clothing
- height
- body proportions
- age
- eye shape

JAX MERCER

Tall athletic young man.

Appearance:
- short dark hair
- intense eyes
- dark blue patterned bandana covering lower face or around neck
- dark fitted T-shirt
- brown / charcoal pants
- strong controlled physique

Personality:
calm strategist,
precise,
rarely panics,
tactical,
protective.

Beginning emotion:
extreme concentration.

Ending emotion:
controlled victory.

Movement:
efficient driving,
minimal wasted motion,
calm body language even during danger.

Keep identical:
- face
- hairstyle
- blue bandana
- shirt
- pants
- height
- proportions
- body build

RICO VALE

Original crime boss character.

Appearance:
- stylish dark hair
- confident narrow eyes
- clean or lightly stubbled face
- dark tailored street jacket
- dark shirt
- restrained jewelry
- smug posture
- sophisticated silhouette

Personality:
arrogant,
calculated,
confident,
believes he has already won.

Beginning emotion:
smug control.

Ending emotion:
surprised and outplayed, but physically unharmed.

Do not make Rico grotesque, psychotic, or horror-like.

[PROP LOCKS]

BLACK DRIVE:
small matte-black rectangular data device.
Same exact shape and size throughout.
It must remain visually readable in Rico’s hand and later Lena’s hand.

JAX’S DEVICE:
small generic dark handheld screen / phone.
During the twist, it displays a simple red warning-style state indicating the drive is disabled.
Avoid recognizable real-world phone branding.

RICO’S PISTOL:
stylized generic dark handgun.
Only used as a visual standoff prop.
No firing.
No bullets.
No injuries.
It is safely knocked away during the confrontation.

MUSCLE CAR:
same dark charcoal muscle car from Part 1.
Wide stance.
Long red rear light treatment.
Strong low silhouette.
No real-world logos.

BLACK SUVs:
large matching dark SUVs.
Consistent shape and scale.
No branding.

[ENVIRONMENT LOCK]

The chase moves through:

1. desert-edge highway
2. narrowing service road / construction route
3. unfinished elevated overpass

Preserve coherent geography.

OVERPASS:
- concrete barriers
- construction barricades
- exposed structural elements
- orange safety lamps
- distant city lights
- dusty road
- dramatic open skyline

Lighting progresses naturally:

bright orange-magenta sunset
→ deeper red-purple dusk
→ cool blue night shadows with warm residual horizon light

[WORLD / ACTION PHYSICS]

All vehicle and character motion should feel grounded and cinematic.

Vehicles must show:
- weight
- suspension movement
- tire grip
- braking momentum
- body roll
- road vibration
- realistic acceleration

No weightless vehicles.
No flying cars.
No impossible spins unless physically motivated.

Action follows:
anticipation → attack → consequence → reaction.

The confrontation is brief and stylized.
No graphic violence.
No prolonged fight choreography.

integrated_multimodal_description:

[SHOT 1] (0.00s–2.10s) — FULL-SPEED PURSUIT

Open immediately on Jax’s dark muscle car accelerating hard down a sunset highway.

Two black SUVs close from behind and another runs ahead.

Tires throw road grit.
Warm sunset reflections streak across the car body.

Cut inside.

Jax grips the wheel firmly, eyes locked forward.

Lena braces in the passenger seat, twists around, and tracks the lead SUV through the side and rear windows.

Camera:
Start with a low rear three-quarter tracking shot beside the muscle car.
Sweep into an aggressive side tracking angle.
Cut to a tight interior two-shot.

Camera speed:
fast but readable.

Emotion:
focused urgency.

Dialogue:

Lena (sharp):
<d>[English] Lead truck. Eleven o’clock.</d>

Jax (calm):
<d>[English] Got it.</d>

Lena:
<d>[English] Don’t lose him.</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] Wasn’t planning to.</d>

Sound:
- deep muscle-car engine
- SUV engines behind
- tire noise
- road rumble
- rushing wind
- seat and clothing movement
- low rhythmic action pulse

Transition:
One SUV moves alongside and begins closing the gap.

[SHOT 2] (2.10s–4.10s) — THE BOX-IN

Two black SUVs attempt to compress Jax’s car between lanes.

Lena leans toward the passenger window and keeps her eyes fixed on the lead SUV.

Her ponytail and red bandana react naturally to airflow.

Jax sees a narrow service lane opening ahead.

He makes one decisive steering move and sends the muscle car through the gap before the SUVs can close it.

The car drops onto the service lane with a heavy suspension compression.

One pursuing SUV follows too aggressively.

Camera:
High rear chase angle showing the box-in formation.
Cut to Lena’s profile tracking the target.
Then low front-quarter shot as Jax cuts across into the service lane.

Emotion:
pressure → opportunity.

Dialogue:

Lena:
<d>[English] They’re boxing us in.</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] Not anymore.</d>

Sound:
- engines climbing
- tire scrub
- brief horn
- gravel spray
- suspension thump
- quick percussion hit as Jax breaks formation

Transition:
The pursuing SUV enters the service road too fast.

[SHOT 3] (4.10s–6.00s) — CONVOY BREAKS

The pursuing SUV clips a lightweight construction barrier.

The barrier spins aside.

The SUV loses alignment and skids safely into additional barricades, sending sparks and dust sideways.

No people are visibly injured.

Jax’s muscle car continues forward.

Ahead, the road rises onto an unfinished elevated overpass.

The lead SUV carrying Rico accelerates toward it.

Camera:
Dynamic low roadside angle as SUV loses control.
Brief wide shot of the impact.
Then long-lens chase composition revealing the overpass ahead.

Emotion:
momentary advantage but increasing danger.

Dialogue:

Lena:
<d>[English] One down.</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] Don’t celebrate yet.</d>

Sound:
- tire squeal
- construction barrel impacts
- metal scrape
- restrained sparks
- engine acceleration
- wind intensifying

Music:
add stronger percussion and low synth pulse.

Transition:
Jax’s car climbs the overpass ramp behind Rico.

[SHOT 4] (6.00s–8.00s) — THE ROAD ENDS

Wide cinematic shot of the unfinished overpass suspended above distant city lights.

Only the lead SUV and Jax’s muscle car remain in the immediate chase.

Rico’s SUV swerves past construction barricades.

A final sharp turn forces the SUV to skid sideways near the unfinished end of the overpass.

Dust rises.

Jax brakes hard and stops several car lengths away.

The muscle car settles on its suspension.

The engines idle.

Suddenly the chase becomes quiet.

Camera:
Aerial-like wide establishing shot over the overpass.
Fast tracking behind both vehicles.
Then controlled side shot of Rico’s SUV skidding.
Slow push toward the two stopped cars.

Emotion:
speed collapses into tense silence.

Dialogue:

Lena (quiet):
<d>[English] End of the road.</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] For somebody.</d>

Sound:
- hard braking
- tire squeal
- gravel sliding
- engine rumble falling to idle
- distant wind
- action music suddenly strips back to a low pulse

Transition:
Rico’s driver door opens.

[SHOT 5] (8.00s–10.10s) — RICO SHOWS HIS HAND

Rico steps from the SUV.

He casually holds the matte-black drive up between two fingers.

In his other hand is a generic stylized handgun held low and controlled.

Lena and Jax exit their muscle car and stand several meters away.

No one rushes.

Rico smiles.

Camera:
Low hero-villain angle on Rico stepping out.
Cut to over-the-shoulder from behind Lena and Jax.
Push into the black drive.
Then medium three-shot.

Emotion:
Rico feels victorious.
Lena looks ready to move.
Jax remains unusually calm.

Dialogue:

Rico (smug):
<d>[English] You two really came all this way for this?</d>

Lena:
<d>[English] Hand it over.</d>

Rico (small laugh):
<d>[English] You were never supposed to leave that station.</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] And yet, here we are.</d>

Sound:
- wind across unfinished concrete
- distant city ambience
- SUV cooling ticks
- fabric movement
- quiet ominous bass tone

Transition:
Rico raises the drive slightly as if showing off his victory.

[SHOT 6] (10.10s–12.10s) — JAX’S TWIST

Jax slowly takes out his small dark handheld device.

The display shows a simple red disabled-state warning.

He tilts it toward Rico.

Rico looks from the device to the black drive.

His smug expression disappears.

Lena’s eyes narrow with a small knowing smile.

Camera:
Close-up of Jax’s calm eyes.
Insert on generic device screen.
Reaction close-up on Rico.
Cut to Lena.

Dialogue:

Rico:
<d>[English] What did you do?</d>

Jax (calm):
<d>[English] The drive was dead before you left the station.</d>

Rico:
<d>[English] You’re bluffing.</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] Try it.</d>

Sound:
- subtle electronic confirmation tone
- wind
- low bass pulse
- tiny musical reversal sting

Important:
The technology is fictional and should not depict real hacking procedures or operational instructions.

Transition:
Rico instinctively looks down at the drive.

That moment creates the opening.

[SHOT 7] (12.10s–13.60s) — THE OPENING

Lena suddenly flicks / tosses a harmless small loose object or handful of construction dust across Rico’s line of sight.

Rico reacts instinctively.

Jax immediately closes the distance.

In one short stylized motion, he redirects Rico’s weapon arm away and the pistol is knocked loose.

The pistol slides safely across the pavement away from everyone.

Lena moves past Rico and takes the black drive.

Rico ends safely on one knee or sitting against a construction barrier, surprised but visibly unharmed.

Do not show detailed combat technique.
Keep the movement fast, cinematic, and impressionistic.

Camera:
Quick lateral whip-follow toward Lena’s distraction.
Low medium shot on Jax’s intervention.
Macro insert of pistol sliding away.
Close shot on Lena securing the drive.

Emotion:
precise teamwork.

Dialogue:

Lena:
<d>[English] Now.</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] Done.</d>

Rico (frustrated):
<d>[English] You think this changes anything?</d>

Sound:
- quick object flick
- cloth movement
- short safe physical impact
- pistol clatter and slide
- drive grab
- restrained cinematic hit

No gunshot.

Transition:
Red-blue light reflections appear far behind them.

[SHOT 8] (13.60s–15.00s) — RECOVERY / HERO EXIT

Lena stands holding the recovered black drive.

Jax joins her.

Rico remains safely behind them, disarmed.

Far down the overpass approach, faint police lights flash in the distance.

Lena and Jax calmly walk toward their waiting muscle car.

The last orange strip of sunset sits beneath a deep violet-blue sky.

Lena looks down at the drive, then toward Jax.

Camera:
Begin medium on Lena holding the drive.
Slow lateral move as Jax joins her.
Then transition into a wide rear hero shot as they walk toward the muscle car.

Final composition:
Lena and Jax walking side by side.
Muscle car ahead.
Red taillights glowing.
City beyond.
Police lights distant.
Rico left behind safely.
Black drive visible in Lena’s hand.

Dialogue:

Lena:
<d>[English] So what’s actually on this thing?</d>

Jax (without turning):
<d>[English] Enough to burn his empire.</d>

Lena gives a small satisfied look.

Sound:
- distant sirens
- soft wind
- slow footsteps on concrete
- car engine idling
- fabric movement
- low triumphant musical rise

Final musical beat lands precisely after Jax’s last line.

CUT TO BLACK.

audio_description:

Generate synchronized native stereo audio.

[CHASE ENVIRONMENT]

- powerful muscle-car engine
- heavier SUV engines
- realistic tires
- road vibration
- wind rush
- suspension compression
- gravel
- construction barrier impacts
- restrained metal scrapes

Do not exaggerate every impact.

[OVERPASS ENVIRONMENT]

- high-altitude wind
- distant city hum
- cooling engines
- occasional construction material rattle
- subtle open-space reverb

[CHARACTER VOICES]

LENA:
young adult female voice.
Low-to-medium register.
Direct.
Confident.
Fast during chase.
Very controlled during confrontation.
No excessive shouting.

JAX:
young adult male.
Low calm voice.
Tactical.
Understated.
Rarely raises volume.
Final line delivered coolly and quietly.

RICO:
adult male.
Smooth confident voice.
Smug at first.
Brief frustration after the disabled-drive reveal.
Never hysterical.

[NON-DIEGETIC MUSIC]

0.00–4.10:
fast restrained electronic-action pulse with low bass and tight percussion.

4.10–6.00:
add sharper rhythmic accents during SUV spin-out.

6.00–8.00:
music reaches peak chase intensity, then rapidly strips down as vehicles stop.

8.00–10.10:
minimal tense bass pulse beneath Rico confrontation.

10.10–12.10:
subtle rising tension with electronic reversal accent during Jax’s reveal.

12.10–13.60:
one fast controlled action hit.

13.60–15.00:
warm low cinematic victory motif, restrained and cool rather than heroic bombast.

Music must remain below dialogue.

[VISUAL STYLE]

Premium cinematic 2D graphic-novel animation.

Visual qualities:
- refined ink outlines
- painterly cel shading
- mature Western graphic-novel aesthetic
- realistic stylized anatomy
- strong silhouettes
- detailed cars
- cinematic road environments
- controlled motion blur
- dramatic rim lighting
- filmic contrast
- rich atmospheric perspective
- subtle texture
- expressive eyes
- consistent faces

Do not make the final footage look like storyboard drawings on paper.

It should look like the storyboard has become a finished high-budget animated action sequence.

[COLOR / LIGHTING PROGRESSION]

0.00–4.00:
strong orange-magenta sunset,
warm body highlights,
cool asphalt shadows.

4.00–8.00:
deeper violet sunset,
red taillights,
orange sparks,
blue-gray road surfaces.

8.00–12.00:
sun nearly gone,
deep purple sky,
warm horizon rim light,
cool shadows around the characters.

12.00–15.00:
blue-violet early night,
remaining orange horizon,
distant red-blue police reflections,
warm red muscle-car taillights.

Keep skin tones natural within the stylized palette.

[CAMERA RULES]

Camera energy progression:

CHASE:
- low rear tracking
- side tracking
- high chase angle
- interior close-ups
- long-lens compression
- dynamic but readable road perspectives

OVERPASS:
- large environmental wides
- controlled brake/skid tracking

STANDOFF:
- slow push-ins
- over-the-shoulders
- clean medium compositions
- reaction close-ups

ACTION BEAT:
- short dynamic lateral move
- one quick controlled whip-follow
- no chaotic cutting

ENDING:
- steady wide hero shot
- slow pull-back or gentle lateral drift

Avoid:
- excessive Dutch angles
- random spinning
- extreme handheld shake
- repetitive zooms
- confusing POV changes
- unreadable cuts

[MOTION / PHYSICS RULES]

Cars:
must feel heavy and grounded.

SUV losing control:
one physically understandable skid into lightweight construction barricades.
No vehicle explosion.
No occupants shown injured.

Ponytail and bandanas:
react naturally to vehicle airflow and wind.

Rico’s pistol:
must never fire.
When disarmed, it clearly falls away from all characters and remains on the ground.

Rico:
must remain visibly safe after the confrontation.

[CONTINUITY RULES]

Maintain perfect continuity from Part 1.

Lena:
same face,
red bandana,
dark cropped shirt,
denim shorts,
high ponytail.

Jax:
same face,
blue/dark bandana,
dark shirt,
brown/dark pants.

Rico:
same face and outfit from his first appearance through final shot.

Black drive:
same matte-black rectangular design.

Muscle car:
same dark body and red rear light treatment.

SUVs:
same consistent dark styling.

Do not regenerate or change vehicles between shots.

Keep environmental geography coherent:
highway → service lane → overpass ramp → unfinished overpass confrontation.

Do not teleport characters or vehicles.

[SAFETY / NEGATIVE CONSTRAINTS]

No blood.
No gore.
No wounds.
No graphic injuries.
No executions.
No torture.
No cruelty.
No civilians harmed.
No explosions.
No gunshots.
No bullet impacts.
No detailed weapon handling instruction.
No prolonged combat choreography.
No realistic depiction of how to disarm a firearm.
Keep the intervention fast, abstract, cinematic, and non-instructional.

No storyboard page visible.
No panel borders.
No timestamps.
No captions.
No production notes.
No subtitles.
No title cards.
No logos.
No brand names.
No watermarks.
No real-world vehicle badges.
No copyrighted characters.
No recognizable game characters.
No extra protagonists.
No random henchmen appearing during final confrontation.
No duplicated Lena.
No duplicated Jax.
No duplicate Rico.
No face changes.
No hairstyle changes.
No wardrobe changes.
No missing bandanas.
No inconsistent drive shape.
No malformed hands.
No extra limbs.
No distorted vehicles.
No teleporting cars.
No random environmental changes.
No hyperreal live action.
No 3D videogame-render appearance.
No anime transformation effects.

FINAL FRAME:

Lena and Jax walk toward their dark muscle car on the unfinished overpass.

Lena holds the recovered black drive.

The red taillights glow ahead.

Faint police lights flash far behind.

The last orange light disappears below the horizon.

Jax has just said:

<d>[English] Enough to burn his empire.</d>

Hold the cinematic hero composition briefly.

CUT TO BLACK.
~~~~

~~~~text
[VIDEO GOAL]

[CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK]

LENA CRUZ:
Young athletic woman with a high dark ponytail, sharp expressive eyes, red patterned bandana covering or resting around her lower face, fitted dark cropped top, light denim shorts, practical shoes, lean athletic proportions.

Simple clean shape.
The REAL black drive is NOT inside the paper bag.
It is seen clearly later in the hand of Rico’s crew near the lead SUV.

FAKE PACKAGE:
Plain brown paper bag.

Inside:
- cheap small green novelty keychain
- folded paper note

The note must clearly communicate:
“TOO SLOW.”

Do not introduce additional items.

MUSCLE CAR:
Dark charcoal/black classic-inspired muscle car.
Wide aggressive stance.
Warm red rear lights.
No logos or recognizable real-world branding.

BLACK SUVs:
Large dark SUVs with bright headlights.
Consistent model and silhouette throughout the video.
No brand badges.

[WORLD / PHYSICS RULES]

The action follows realistic cinematic cause-and-effect.

Every movement must have:
anticipation → action → physical consequence → reaction.

Examples:
- headlights appear → Jax notices → body turns → he warns Lena
- Lena realizes trap → immediately creates cover
- Jax identifies escape route → forces open rear exit
- they reach car → Lena spots black drive → Jax accelerates

No random explosions.
No supernatural movement.
No teleporting.
No impossible weapon behavior.
No excessive debris.

Keep action grounded, readable, and fast.

integrated_multimodal_description:

[SHOT 1] (0.00s–2.20s) — THE PICKUP

Start with a wide cinematic exterior of the isolated roadside gas station under a dramatic orange-magenta sunset.

Long shadows cross slightly wet asphalt.
The convenience-store interior glows with cool fluorescent light.
The station canopy is illuminated against the colorful dusk sky.

Lena and Jax walk toward the station together from the foreground.

As they approach the pumps, they stop.

Jax slowly scans the perimeter, checking parked vehicles, road entrances, and reflections in the store windows.

Lena adjusts the red bandana over her lower face and looks toward the convenience-store door.

She gives Jax a tiny confirming glance.

Jax gives a subtle nod.

Lena separates from him and walks toward the store.

Camera:
Begin with a low wide-angle establishing dolly toward them.
Transition into a medium two-shot.
Subtle arc around Lena as she turns toward the entrance.

Emotion:
quiet professionalism with underlying suspicion.

Dialogue:

Jax (low controlled voice):
<d>[English] Two minutes. If anything feels wrong, walk.</d>

Lena (dry, confident):
<d>[English] You worry too much.</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] That’s why we’re still alive.</d>

Sound:
- distant highway ambience
- light desert wind
- faint fuel-pump hum
- shoes on asphalt
- distant car pass
- soft fabric movement
- subtle atmospheric thriller pulse begins

Transition:
Follow Lena through the glass entrance.

[SHOT 2] (2.20s–4.40s) — THE HANDOFF

Inside the convenience store.

The lighting shifts from warm sunset orange outside to cool desaturated fluorescent interior light.

Lena walks toward the counter.

The cashier watches her nervously.

His eyes briefly flick toward the front windows before returning to Lena.

Lena notices this.

The cashier slowly slides a plain brown paper bag across the counter.

Lena does not immediately grab it.

She studies the cashier.

He avoids eye contact.

Then Lena takes the bag.

Camera:
Medium tracking shot behind Lena entering the store.
Cut to cashier’s anxious close-up.
Insert close-up of the brown paper bag sliding across the counter.
Slow push toward Lena’s eyes.

Emotion:
suspicion increasing.

Dialogue:

Lena (quiet):
<d>[English] Where’s Vale?</d>

Cashier (nervous, whispering):
<d>[English] I... I was only told to give you the bag.</d>

Lena:
<d>[English] Told by who?</d>

The cashier says nothing.

Sound:
- fluorescent electrical hum
- refrigerator compressor
- paper bag scraping across countertop
- subtle nervous breath from cashier
- distant exterior traffic

Music:
Minimal low electronic pulse, restrained.

Transition:
Lena opens the bag.

[SHOT 3] (4.40s–6.20s) — TOO SLOW

Tight insert inside the paper bag.

Instead of the black drive:
a cheap green novelty keychain and folded paper note.

Lena pulls out the note.

She unfolds it.

The message reads:
“TOO SLOW.”

Hold just long enough to understand.

Cut to extreme close-up of Lena’s eyes above the red bandana.

Her expression immediately changes from suspicion to certainty.

She looks toward the front windows.

Camera:
Macro insert inside bag.
Match cut from unfolded paper to Lena’s eyes.
Very slow push-in.

Emotion:
realization.

Dialogue:

Lena (under her breath):
<d>[English] Damn it.</d>

Cashier (frightened):
<d>[English] I’m sorry.</d>

Sound:
- paper unfolding
- subtle bass hit when message is revealed
- refrigerator hum suddenly feels louder
- tiny room-tone drop immediately before Lena reacts

Transition:
Hard motivated cut from Lena’s eyes to bright headlights outside.

[SHOT 4] (6.20s–8.40s) — THE TRAP CLOSES

Exterior from Jax’s perspective.

Multiple black SUVs turn into the gas station from different entrances.

Headlights flare directly toward him.

One SUV swings across the driveway.
Another rolls near the pumps.
A third blocks the road exit.

Jax instantly understands.

His relaxed posture snaps into tactical alertness.

He turns toward the convenience store.

Camera:
Over-the-shoulder behind Jax facing approaching SUVs.
Fast controlled push-in as headlights appear.
Brief low-angle shot of tires turning into the station.
Return to Jax.

Dialogue:

<d>[English] Lena! Move!</d>

Inside, Lena immediately turns toward his voice.

Lena:
<d>[English] I knew it.</d>

Sound:
- SUV engines approaching
- tire grit
- doors beginning to open
- Jax’s shout
- store door bell rattling
- thriller percussion enters

Transition:

[SHOT 5] (8.40s–10.80s) — BREAK THE TRAP

Inside the store, Lena reacts instantly.

She grabs the edge of a lightweight product display and shoves it sideways, creating visual cover between herself and the front windows.

Packaged goods scatter across the floor.

No one is struck.

At the same time outside, Jax moves along the side of the building toward a rear service exit.

He shoulders or kicks the jammed rear door near the latch.

The door bursts outward.

Use a rapid intercut rhythm between Lena and Jax.

Lena sprints toward the rear.

Jax reaches through and pulls the door wide for her.

Camera:
Dynamic medium tracking with Lena.
Low angle as display moves.
Cut to Jax moving beside brick wall.
Close shot on his boot/body impacting the jammed door.
Wide rear-exit reveal.

Emotion:
rapid controlled survival response.

Dialogue:

Sound:

- SUV doors outside

No gunfire is necessary.
The threat should come from the trap and incoming crew, not graphic violence.

Transition:
Lena bursts through the rear door past Jax.

[SHOT 6] (10.80s–12.80s) — RUN FOR THE CAR

Lena and Jax sprint together behind the station.

Industrial rear lot:
chain-link fence,
service barrels,
wet asphalt,
dumpsters,
utility pipes,
warm sunset beyond the rooftops.

Alarm light flashes intermittently across the walls.

Their dark muscle car is parked ahead.

Jax runs slightly toward the driver side.
Lena heads toward passenger side.

Camera:

Brief rear tracking shot toward the car.
Natural speed blur at frame edges while keeping faces and silhouettes readable.

<d>[English] You’re looking at it.</d>

- alarm chirp
- distant SUV engines

[SHOT 7] (12.80s–15.00s) — THE REAL DRIVE / CLIFFHANGER

Lena glances backward.

Across the rear service lot, one of Rico’s crew is stepping into the lead black SUV.

In his hand:
the REAL matte-black drive.

Make the black drive clearly visible for a brief readable moment against the sunset/headlight contrast.

Lena’s eyes lock onto it.

Jax is already in the driver seat and starts the muscle car.

Lena dives into the passenger side.

The engine roars.

The lead SUV accelerates away.

Jax launches after it.

The muscle car swings out from behind the station and begins pursuit as other black SUVs move behind them.

Finish with a powerful rear three-quarter chase composition:
muscle car accelerating into the sunset,
red taillights glowing,
black SUVs ahead and behind,
dust and road grit lifting.

Include a brief insert of Lena’s determined eyes reflected in the side mirror.

Dialogue:

<d>[English] Jax—the drive!</d>

Jax:
<d>[English] I see it.</d>

Lena:
<d>[English] They still have it.</d>

Jax (calm):

Sound:

- door slam
- tire chirp
- accelerating muscle-car engine
- SUV engines
- gravel scattering
- wind building
- final rising cinematic pulse

End exactly as the pursuit begins.

Do NOT show the highway chase beyond the first acceleration.
~~~~

## 32. Create a breathtaking 15-second cinematic flower-morph sequence using the seven

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/create-a-breathtaking-15-second-cinematic-flower-morph-sequence-using-622337/video-05344071a617.webm">

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Create a breathtaking 15-second cinematic flower-morph sequence using the seven uploaded flower images as consecutive visual references: Image 1: white calla lily Image 2: coral...</summary>

~~~~text
Create a breathtaking 15-second cinematic flower-morph sequence using the seven uploaded flower images as consecutive visual references:

Image 1: white calla lily
Image 2: coral hibiscus
Image 3: burgundy dahlia
Image 4: orange and cobalt-blue bird of paradise
Image 5: neon-yellow orchid
Image 6: deep-purple iris
Image 7: electric-blue Himalayan poppy

MOTION:
One continuous cinematic macro shot. The camera performs a slow, controlled push toward the center of the flower while every transformation happens clearly in full view. The flower remains centered against a deep, pure black background. Petals rotate, flutter, stretch, divide, multiply, fold, and physically reorganize into each new botanical form. Translucent long-exposure trails follow the movement like illuminated silk ribbons. The single stem remains visually anchored throughout the sequence.

ACTION AND TIMING:

0–2 seconds:
A white calla lily rotates gracefully. Its sculptural trumpet petal unfurls, creating sweeping trails of pearl white, silver, and champagne.

2–4 seconds:
The calla lily’s curved petal organically divides into five broad petals, becoming a coral hibiscus. The golden spadix elongates into the hibiscus stamen while coral, peach, and white trails spiral outward.

4–6 seconds:
The hibiscus petals rapidly multiply from the center and fold into hundreds of geometric burgundy and raspberry petals. The flower becomes a richly layered dahlia, forming a hypnotic rotating vortex.

6–8 seconds:
The dahlia petals sharpen, extend, and reorganize into the architectural form of a bird of paradise. Burgundy and magenta ignite into fiery orange while cobalt-blue and turquoise structures burst outward like exotic wings.

8–10 seconds:
The bird of paradise curls inward. Its orange structures soften and divide into the broad neon-yellow petals of an orchid. The cobalt-blue structures transform into the intricate orchid center, accented with crimson and gold. Yellow, chartreuse, and luminous-white trails pulse outward.

10–12 seconds:
The orchid petals stretch and twist, transitioning from neon yellow through electric violet into deep royal purple. Golden markings expand into the textured beard of a majestic iris. The iris opens dramatically as violet, lavender, and golden trails circle around it.

12–15 seconds:
At the visual climax, the deep-purple iris spins faster. Its sculptural petals soften into delicate translucent fabric while the violet color shifts through indigo into intense electric blue. The iris beard separates into hundreds of tiny golden particles that spiral around the flower’s center.

The flowing petals broaden and physically transform into the delicate, paper-thin petals of an electric-blue Himalayan poppy. Its intricate golden-yellow stamens and pale-green pistil assemble from the swirling particles and snap into razor-sharp focus.

The poppy opens fully as a final wave of cobalt-blue, turquoise, icy-white, and silvery long-exposure trails expands outward like luminous wings. The trails gently return into the petals, leaving the electric-blue Himalayan poppy completely still, perfectly formed, and razor-sharp in the final frame.

RHYTHM:
Begin elegant and controlled, then progressively increase the speed, scale, and complexity of each transformation. Build through strong color contrasts and reach maximum energy during the purple-iris-to-electric-blue-poppy transformation. End with a brief moment of complete stillness so the final flower can be clearly admired.

STYLE:
Photo-realistic premium macro cinematography, extreme botanical detail, physically believable organic morphing, dramatic side-lighting, deep cinematic contrast, translucent petals, luminous long-exposure trails, controlled volumetric particles, elegant motion blur, luxurious fine-art photography, pure black background, clean centered composition, rich natural colors, high-end commercial quality, 4K.

IMPORTANT:
Create genuine continuous anatomical flower transformations. No cuts, crossfades, dissolves, overlays, double exposure, image blending, or separate flower shots. Every flower must physically grow from the petals, center, and botanical structures of the previous flower. Preserve one continuous stem, stable central positioning, consistent scale, coherent lighting, and uninterrupted forward camera movement. Every flower must remain clearly recognizable before transforming. No additional flowers, bouquet, vase, hands, insects, text, borders, or background objects.
~~~~

</details>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Prompt [1] FORMAT 10-second 16:9 cinematic motion-graphics promo built from a single desert muscle-car image. Premium, fast, dimensional, elegant, and tactile. [2] ASSETS Use...</summary>

~~~~text
Prompt

[1] FORMAT
10-second 16:9 cinematic motion-graphics promo built from a single desert muscle-car image. Premium, fast, dimensional, elegant, and tactile.

[2] ASSETS
Use Image 1 as the exact first-frame source and core visual reference for the vintage white muscle car, dusty desert road, rock formations, muted sand palette, and gritty film texture.

[3] IDENTITY
Preserve:
- the old white Dodge Charger–style muscle car as the hero subject
- weathered paint, rust marks, dust buildup, and worn wheels
- low front three-quarter angle and dominant car scale
- barren desert highway with cracked asphalt and rocky outcrops
- beige, sepia, and charcoal color family
- airborne dust and dry wind atmosphere
- gritty, premium editorial mood rather than cheerful commercial energy
- realistic hard sunlight and soft dust diffusion
- grounded reflections and believable shadow direction

[4] BEATS
[0–2 seconds] Start from the original composition. Create subtle stillness for half a second, then introduce layered parallax: foreground dust, midground car, and background rocks separate in depth. Camera begins a slow push-in with a slight rightward arc. Fine sand drifts across frame.
[2–4 seconds] Speed ramps in. Add dimensional motion-graphic accents: thin metallic lines, soft grid fragments, and subtle contour-tracing highlights briefly wrap around the car body, emphasizing shape and speed. Main title appears: "BORN FROM DUST".
[4–6 seconds] Camera performs a faster sweep past the front quarter panel toward the side, with strong depth, dust streaks, and passing light sweeps. Secondary text appears: "SPEED HAS A SHAPE". Keep the car stable and heroic while the environment feels fast.
[6–8 seconds] Push the most dynamic moment: dust bursts, perspective stretch, and elegant 3D typography planes moving in space around the car without blocking it. Add one short accent line: "RAW / FAST / ICONIC".
[8–10 seconds] Motion resolves into a premium final frame. Camera eases back into a clean hero composition. Final end card text appears and holds: "CHASE THE HORIZON". Keep the last 1.5 seconds readable and polished.

[5] CAMERA
Cinematic virtual camera from a single still image. Use layered 2.5D depth separation, push-ins, slight orbit motion, and one speed-ramped sweep for dimensionality. Start as a low wide hero shot, move into a medium close pass on the car, then settle into a clean final hero frame. Motion should feel expensive and controlled, not chaotic.

[6] LIGHT
Harsh desert sunlight with dusty diffusion. Palette: sand beige, sun-faded ivory, smoky gray, and soft metallic charcoal. Add tasteful specular sweeps on the car edges and grille. Film texture should feel editorial and refined, with fine grain and suspended dust particles for depth.

[7] EDIT
Use seamless motion-graphics transitions only, no hard scene changes. Typography and graphic elements should feel integrated with the car and dust flow. Text behavior:
- "BORN FROM DUST" enters with a fast horizontal build and slight depth extrusion
- it remains readable for around 1 second
- it exits by dissolving into dust particles
- do not warp, misspell, mirror, or replace the text
- "SPEED HAS A SHAPE" enters on a perspective plane aligned to the car motion
- "RAW / FAST / ICONIC" appears as small premium kinetic type
- final text "CHASE THE HORIZON" enters cleanly, stays stable, and fades softly at the end

[8] AUDIO
Audio: dry desert wind, fine sand skimming across metal, distant low engine rumble, and one sharp whoosh accent at 4 seconds during the speed ramp.
BGM: 10-second cue, 65% cinematic tension and 35% luxury fashion energy. Deep pulse, restrained synth bass, tight percussion, airy reverse textures, and metallic hits. Build gradually from 0–4 seconds, intensify from 4–8 seconds, then resolve into a confident sustained ending. Freeze the final 2 seconds on a clean, premium sonic tail. Do not imitate an existing melody.

[9] NEGATIVE
No subtitles, on-screen text beyond the specified titles, watermarks, platform logos, or stickers.
Do not introduce Chinese text, garbled characters, or misspellings.
No slideshow feeling — use natural dimensional camera motion and seamless transitions.
No cartoon styling, no cheap VFX clutter, no excessive lens distortion, no unrealistic car deformation, and no extra vehicles or people.
~~~~

</details>

<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action, cinematic, photorealistic with true fine pores and real skin texture, no beauty filter. A full-body side-on wide shot of a...</summary>

~~~~text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action, cinematic, photorealistic with true fine pores and real skin texture, no beauty filter. A full-body side-on wide shot of a 25-year-old woman with cool fair skin, dark almond eyes and long straight black hair, walking steadily from left to right along a colonnade of stone columns on a city street. Her face is identical in every era of the film; only her clothing, her hair styling and the world behind her change. Her stride never changes speed and she never looks at the camera until the final moment. The camera trucks right with large amplitude at slow speed, travelling alongside her for the entire film with no other camera movement and no change of direction. Foreground columns sweep across the lens as the camera moves, and each time a column wipes the frame the world behind her changes to the next era.

The film opens in the 1920s: low-saturation sepia light, gas lamps, a horse-drawn carriage, a beaded drop-waist dress and a cloche hat. At 00:03.000 a column wipes past and the world becomes the 1950s: warm amber light, a pastel car at the kerb, a chrome diner sign, a full-skirted dress and red lipstick. At 00:06.000 the next column wipes past and the world becomes the 1980s: dense pink and cyan neon reflecting off wet asphalt, a padded-shoulder jacket, heavy dark night air. At 00:09.000 the next column wipes past and the world becomes the present day: cool white daylight, glass office towers, passers-by lit by phone screens, an oversized wool coat. At 00:12.000 the last column wipes past and the world becomes the near future: pale blue light, floating holographic advertisements drifting between the columns, a sleek structured coat with a faint luminous seam.

At 00:14.000 she stops walking. The camera comes to rest with her. She turns her head and looks straight into the lens for the final beat, holding still until the end. No on-screen text or subtitles at any point.

overall_soundscape: Her footsteps strike the stone at exactly the same steady pace from the first frame to the last, the one sound that never changes. Behind her the street swaps out at each era: horse hooves and a distant bell, then car horns and chattering pedestrians, then heavy rain on asphalt and a low traffic drone, then a modern city hum, then an almost silent electric whirr. Each column wipe is marked by a short dry rush of air across the lens.

non_diegetic_music: A steady four-beat pulse at a constant tempo runs from the first frame to the last, gaining one new instrument at every era change: a lone bass drum, then upright bass, then analogue synth, then layered electronic percussion, then a wide sustained synth chord. It reaches full volume as she stops and cuts off on her final look.
~~~~

</details>

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — ## Concept AQUARELLE No.7 — an avant-garde haute couture film where watercolor becomes a living medium. 15-second cinematic sequence. The rhythm controls the visual world: 0–4s:...</summary>

~~~~text
## Concept
AQUARELLE No.7 — an avant-garde haute couture film where watercolor becomes a living medium.

15-second cinematic sequence.

The rhythm controls the visual world:
0–4s: restrained silence and negative space
4–8s: gradual density buildup
8s: drop moment, expanding into fluid long-form motion
12–15s: transition into a final fashion poster composition

## Character Identity Lock

Maintain the exact same female character throughout the entire video.

Identity:
- Young woman
- Long black hair
- Calm and refined facial features
- White high-neck pigment dress
- Black wide belt
- Pigment heels

Strict consistency:
- Same face
- Same hairstyle
- Same age
- Same body proportions
- Same garment structure

Any new colors must only appear through watercolor gradually absorbing into the fabric.

Core concept:
Her fingertips can extract transparent watercolor ribbons from the air.

These watercolor ribbons can:
- Pull the camera through space
- Transform the environment
- Shape physical typography
- Interact with depth and materials

The world follows real cinematic physics:
water, paper, fabric, light, shadows, and depth of field must feel physically believable.

## Camera Direction

Strict one-take shot.
No cuts.
No teleportation.
No hidden transitions.

Camera journey:

Macro shot of a floating water droplet →
watercolor ribbon emerges →
camera pulls back to reveal the woman →
camera circles around her →
enters a paper art gallery →
passes through dimensional typography →
rises into a final overhead fashion poster.

## Typography

Only allow these words:

LET SILENCE BLOOM

AQUARELLE No.7

WEAR THE UNSEEN

Typography is not a flat overlay.

Letters must have:
- Physical depth
- Wet watercolor reflections
- Paper fiber edges
- Shadows
- Occlusion
- Material interaction

## Visual Style

Avant-garde fashion editorial.

Inspired by:
- Museum catalog composition
- Handmade ivory paper
- Sculptural negative space
- Elegant Didone serif typography
- Translucent watercolor calligraphy

Color palette:
- Pale cyan
- Crimson lake
- Smoky violet
- Ink black

## Motion Language

Every movement follows the music:

Kick:
Camera movement and paper folding.

Wooden snare:
Paper structures physically fold and transform.

Sub-bass:
Changes the perception of spatial scale.

##  Storyboard

30 beats, 0.5 seconds each.

01 0.0–0.5
Macro shot: A transparent water droplet floats in the air, reflecting a blurred silhouette of a black-haired woman.

02 0.5–1.0
The droplet stretches with the breath-like vocal, becoming a pale cyan watercolor thread.

03 1.0–1.5
Camera travels backward along the thread as paper fibers slowly emerge into focus.

04 1.5–2.0
The thread wraps around the lens. Focus shifts to her raised fingertip.

05 2.0–3.0
Camera continues pulling back, revealing her face and white high-neck dress.

06 3.0–4.0
She moves her wrist. The watercolor thread guides a smooth camera arc.

07 4.0–8.0
Additional watercolor colors emerge from her movement.
Paper folds, typography begins forming, and the phrase "LET SILENCE BLOOM" appears as a physical object.

08 8.0–12.0
The camera passes through a transparent paper flower structure.
The environment expands into an endless ivory paper gallery.

Her dress absorbs watercolor naturally.
The ribbons create sculptural forms around her body.

09 12.0–15.0
Camera cranes upward.

The composition transforms into a luxury fashion advertisement poster.

Typography appears:

AQUARELLE No.7

WEAR THE UNSEEN

Final frame:
A museum-level fashion editorial poster.
The woman remains centered, calm, and elegant.
A final watercolor droplet remains suspended in the air.

## Negative Prompt

No:
- Cuts
- Scene changes
- Identity change
- Face swap
- Extra limbs
- Deformed hands
- Random costume changes
- Explosive paint effects without physical cause
- Incorrect typography
- Chinese characters
- Extra subtitles
- Extra logos
- Watermarks
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Audio: Crisp female anime idol voice clearly pronouncing "みんな、こんにちは！今日も一緒に楽しもうね！" in Japanese. Underneath, an upbeat synth-pop idol BGM playing, accompanied by a soft microphone "pop" sound at 0.5s and a sparkling magic SFX hit at 3.5s during the pose.
~~~~

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect Ratio: 16:9 | Style: Authentic UGC / iPhone selfie-vlog, handheld, natural light, slight motion blur, TikTok/Reels energy — NOT cinematic. Product...</summary>

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect Ratio: 16:9 | Style: Authentic UGC / iPhone selfie-vlog, handheld, natural light, slight motion blur, TikTok/Reels energy — NOT cinematic.
Product Reference: Use the uploaded ramen bowl image as the only product reference. Keep the broth color, noodle thickness, toppings, egg placement, chashu slices, and bowl appearance identical throughout every shot.
Camera: iPhone 15 Pro handheld with front/back camera switching, realistic autofocus hunting, natural handheld movement, slight exposure shifts, warm apartment lighting.
Character Description
Name: Yuna
A young Japanese woman in her early 20s with soft natural beauty, shoulder-length dark hair tied loosely, subtle makeup, oversized gray hoodie, cheerful personality, filming casually inside a cozy apartment kitchen.
Shot Breakdown
SHOT 1 (0–2s) — Selfie holding the ramen bowl.
Dialogue: "Late-night ramen again."
SHOT 2 (2–4s) — Overhead reveal with steam rising.
SHOT 3 (4–6s) — Close-up autofocus on noodles and broth.
SHOT 4 (6–8s) — Chopsticks lifting noodles with a natural noodle stretch.
SHOT 5 (8–10s) — Selfie bite reaction.
Dialogue: "That's insanely good."
SHOT 6 (10–12s) — Casual b-roll of the bowl while she reaches for another bite.
SHOT 7 (12–14s) — Selfie holding noodles toward camera.
Dialogue: "Worth every bite."
~~~~

~~~~text
CUT6 0:10-0:12

CUT7 0:12-0:14

CUT8 0:14-0:15

番号、見出し、解説文、配色パレット、商品比較図を表示しない
ポスター、絵コンテ、広告ボード、資料画像のようにしない
~~~~

## 58. KALDR cold perfume commercial multi-reference

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/kaldr-cold-perfume-commercial-multi-reference-943073/video-0b03def784aa.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/kaldr-cold-perfume-commercial-multi-reference-943073/poster-b0ced00e36d5.jpg" alt="KALDR cold perfume commercial multi-reference video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — REFERENCE USAGE: Image 1 — the KALDR bottle. Preserve the smoked charcoal glass, bevelled edges, pale blue liquid, brushed gunmetal cap and the etched silver KALDR mark exactly....</summary>

~~~~text
REFERENCE USAGE:
Image 1 — the KALDR bottle. Preserve the smoked charcoal glass, bevelled edges, pale blue liquid, brushed gunmetal cap and the etched silver KALDR mark exactly.
Image 2 — the closing brand lockup. Preserve the letterforms, tracking, silver finish and the COLD KEEPS line exactly. It appears only in the final shot, alone on black.
Image 3 — the location, palette, cloud, surf and film texture for the whole film.
Image 4 — a three panel character sheet of the woman. Use it only to match her face, hair, frost detail and black greatcoat. Do not reproduce the sheet's panel layout, its grey backdrop or its flat studio lighting. She must appear as a single person inside the Image 3 environment, lit by that location.
Audio 1 — the voice for the woman's spoken line. Match its timbre, pitch, accent, breathiness and pacing exactly. Use it only for her voice, not as background audio or ambience.

Premium luxury fragrance brand film. Photoreal 35mm anamorphic, high contrast, fine grain, cold specular highlights, deep crushed blacks. Palette strictly black sand, gunmetal, glacial blue and silver, with no warm tones anywhere. Setting: a black volcanic beach on a northern coast at blue hour under heavy storm cloud. Tone: severe, violent, expensive. This is a fast, aggressive fashion edit, not a calm product film. Never slow, never ceremonial, never a slideshow of pretty holds.

[0-1.5s] Cold open, no establishing shot. Camera at water level, lens half submerged, [Static shot]. A sheet of freezing surf races straight at the lens. Her boots stride hard into frame through the water, coat hem whipping. HARD CUT.

[1.5-2.6s] Extreme macro insert. Frost crystals race across black glass and flood the etched KALDR mark with rime, filling the letters. HARD CUT.

[2.6-4.2s] Behind her, low and close, [Tracking shot] pushing fast. She walks straight into an oncoming wave that towers over her, coat streaming sideways, never breaking stride. HARD CUT.

[4.2-5.4s] Reverse, low angle from the water. The wave collapses over her. Camera [Shake] on the impact. Cut on the peak of the white spray. HARD CUT.

[5.4-9s] SLOW MOTION. The wave freezes solid around her mid collapse. She stands inside a shell of blue black ice, the spray suspended in the air as a thousand hanging shards, her coat frozen mid snap, her face calm and unbothered. Camera [Tracking shot] orbits right through the suspended shards, holding her at centre. This is the hero image of the film.

[9-9.8s] Snap back to real time. The ice shell detonates outward. Camera [Pull out] fast and hard through the exploding shards. HARD CUT.

[9.8-12.6s] Tight close up, [Push in]. She walks out of the collapsing ice straight at the lens, ice water running off her coat, looking directly into camera. She says quietly: "I was never meant to melt." She holds the look for a beat after the line. HARD CUT.

[12.6-13.8s] The bottle alone, upright on wet black sand, shot low and close on a macro lens. Frost blooms outward from its base toward the lens. No text of any kind in this shot. HARD CUT.

[13.8-15s] Pure black frame. The brand lockup from Image 2 appears alone, centred, exactly as in the reference. The wordmark KALDR emerges from darkness at low opacity, letter spacing opening over 0.4 seconds into sharp focus with a faint edge glow, and the line COLD KEEPS sits smaller beneath it. Nothing else is in this frame. Critical: the bottle, the woman, the beach and the wordmark must never share a frame. The lockup must never overlap or be occluded by the bottle, and the etched mark on the glass must never appear in the same shot as the lockup. Hold the lockup to the end.

Dialogue and lip sync: only one line is spoken, in the shot beginning at 9.8 seconds: "I was never meant to melt." Match the voice in Audio 1 exactly, including its timbre, breathiness, pacing and the way the sentence lands without rising at the end. Do not substitute a different voice, do not add reverb or processing that changes its character. Her lip movement must match the words frame accurately and stop when the line ends. Her breath fogs on the words. Do not repeat the line and do not add any other spoken words anywhere in the film.

Pacing: hold every cut until its stated timecode and do not shorten the last two shots. The first four shots are fast and aggressive. The slow motion shot is the only long hold in the film. Everything after it is fast again.

Audio: hard coastal wind, heavy surf, a low sub bass drone. At 5.8 seconds, one deep impact hit, a splintering glass freeze crackle and a reversed swell. Then cut the wind, the surf and all low end to complete silence from 6 seconds to 9 seconds, leaving only isolated ice ticks and faint crystal ring in that window. This silence is mandatory and must be obvious. At 9 seconds the full mix slams back in with a hard low kick and the crash of shattering ice into water. As she speaks, duck the wind, surf and low end so her voice sits forward and dry. The final second holds one sustained low string chord decaying into wind. No voiceover, no narrator, no lyrics, no recognisable melody.

Do not add other people, boats, buildings, vehicles, modern objects, subtitles, on screen captions, watermarks, or any text other than specified. Do not warp or distort the bottle or the wordmark. No cartoon or plastic CGI water or ice. Keep skin texture real, no over smoothing. Never let the ice or spray form a face, eyes, or a creature.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
日本語での会話シーン。ピンクシャツを着た泥棒の男が、おばあさんに見つかり、こっぴどく叱られて立ちすくんでいる。男はそばにいる少女に向かって言い訳を始める。「別に盗むつもりじゃなかったんだ、ちょっと借りただけだったんだよ」と弁解していると、おばあさんがふと思い出したように話を遮り、「そういえば、はいるお スリーが出たんだって」と告知するように明るく言う。少女と男は一瞬きょとんとした表情を浮かべ、泥棒は「そそ、そうだよ！はいるおスリーが出たんだよ！そのためにパンをちょっと借りたんだよ！」と気まずい空気が笑いに変わる。少女は画面に向かって軽くアピールするような仕草を見せ、まるでコマーシャルの一言のように場の空気を切り替える。カメラは9種類の異なるアングルで切り替わり、男が言い訳する肩越しショット、少女が告知する瞬間のクローズアップ、おばあさんと男が驚く表情のアップ、三人の位置関係が分かる引きの全体ショットでしめくくる。コミカルな緊張感と告知の明るさを両立させるように構成される。
~~~~
